==================================================
FILE: backend\server.js

import 'dotenv/config'; // harus paling atas agar env terbaca sebelum modul lain dimuat
import express from 'express';
import cors from 'cors';
import authRoutes from './routes/authRoutes.js';
import orderRoutes from './routes/orderRoutes.js';
import driverRoutes from './routes/driverRoutes.js';
import spotRoutes from './routes/spotRoutes.js';

if (!process.env.JWT_SECRET || process.env.JWT_SECRET.length < 32) {
  throw new Error('JWT_SECRET wajib diisi di .env (minimal 32 karakter).');
}

const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;

// CLIENT_URL boleh berisi beberapa origin dipisah koma.
// Default mencakup port dev Vite di vite.config.js (5175) dan default Vite (5173).
const allowedOrigins = (
  process.env.CLIENT_URL || 'http://localhost:5175,http://localhost:5173'
)
  .split(',')
  .map((o) => o.trim())
  .filter(Boolean);

app.use(
  cors({
    origin: (origin, callback) => {
      if (!origin || allowedOrigins.includes(origin)) return callback(null, true);
      return callback(new Error('Origin tidak diizinkan oleh CORS.'));
    },
    credentials: true,
  })
);
app.use(express.json({ limit: '100kb' }));

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'API AnjemDips berjalan normal!',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  });
});

app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/orders', orderRoutes);
app.use('/api/driver', driverRoutes);
app.use('/api/spots', spotRoutes);

app.use((req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json({
    success: false,
    message: 'Endpoint tidak ditemukan.',
  });
});

// Penanganan error global (JSON rusak, CORS, dsb.)
// eslint-disable-next-line no-unused-vars
app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
  const status = err.type === 'entity.parse.failed' ? 400 : err.status || 500;
  res.status(status).json({
    success: false,
    message:
      status === 400 ? 'Format request tidak valid.' : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
  });
});

if (!process.env.VERCEL) {
  app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`Server AnjemDips berjalan di http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  });
}

export default app;

import 'dotenv/config';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const { SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_KEY } = process.env;

if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_KEY) {
  throw new Error(
    'SUPABASE_URL dan SUPABASE_KEY wajib diisi di file .env backend.'
  );
}

const supabase = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_KEY, {
  auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
});

export default supabase;


==================================================
FILE: backend\controllers\authController.js
==================================================
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';

const USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS =
  'id, nama, nim, email, no_wa, role, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang, is_online, created_at';

const ACTIVE_ORDER_STATUSES = ['pending', 'accepted', 'on_progress'];

const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

const generateToken = (user) => {
  return jwt.sign(
    {
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      role: user.role,
      nama: user.nama,
    },
    process.env.JWT_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: '7d' }
  );
};

// POST /api/auth/register
export const register = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      nama,
      nim,
      email,
      password,
      no_wa,
      role,
      jenis_kendaraan,
      plat_nomor,
      kapasitas_penumpang,
    } = req.body;

    if (!nama || !email || !password || !no_wa) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Nama, email, password, dan nomor WhatsApp wajib diisi.',
      });
    }

    if (
      [nama, email, password, no_wa].some((v) => typeof v !== 'string') ||
      (nim !== undefined && nim !== null && typeof nim !== 'string')
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format data registrasi tidak valid.',
      });
    }

    const cleanNimInput = nim ? nim.trim() : '';
    if (cleanNimInput && !/^\d{8,20}$/.test(cleanNimInput)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'NIM harus berupa angka 8â€“20 digit.',
      });
    }

    if (!/^(\+?62|0)8\d{7,12}$/.test(no_wa.trim())) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format nomor WhatsApp tidak valid (contoh: 081234567890).',
      });
    }

    if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(email.trim())) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format email tidak valid.',
      });
    }

    if (password.length < 6) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Password minimal terdiri dari 6 karakter.',
      });
    }

    const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const validRole = role === 'driver' ? 'driver' : 'customer';

    let cleanJenisKendaraan = null;
    let cleanPlatNomor = null;
    let cleanKapasitas = null;

    if (validRole === 'driver') {
      if (
        jenis_kendaraan !== undefined &&
        jenis_kendaraan !== null &&
        String(jenis_kendaraan).trim() !== ''
      ) {
        const normalizedVehicle = String(jenis_kendaraan).trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!['motor', 'mobil'].includes(normalizedVehicle)) {
          return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: 'Jenis kendaraan harus berupa "motor" atau "mobil".',
          });
        }
        cleanJenisKendaraan = normalizedVehicle;
      }

      if (
        plat_nomor !== undefined &&
        plat_nomor !== null &&
        String(plat_nomor).trim() !== ''
      ) {
        cleanPlatNomor = String(plat_nomor).trim().toUpperCase();
      }

      if (
        cleanJenisKendaraan === 'mobil' &&
        kapasitas_penumpang !== undefined &&
        kapasitas_penumpang !== null &&
        kapasitas_penumpang !== ''
      ) {
        const parsedKapasitas = Number(kapasitas_penumpang);
        if (!Number.isInteger(parsedKapasitas) || parsedKapasitas < 1) {
          return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: 'Kapasitas penumpang harus berupa angka minimal 1.',
          });
        }
        cleanKapasitas = parsedKapasitas;
      }

      // Motor selalu berkapasitas 1 (konsisten dengan updateDriverVehicle)
      if (cleanJenisKendaraan === 'motor') {
        cleanKapasitas = 1;
      }
    }

    const { data: existingUser, error: checkError } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .select('id')
      .eq('email', normalizedEmail)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (checkError) throw checkError;

    if (existingUser) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'NIM sudah terdaftar. Silakan login.',
      });
    }

    const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);

    const { data: newUser, error: insertError } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .insert([
        {
          nama: nama.trim(),
          nim: cleanNimInput || null,
          email: normalizedEmail,
          password: hashedPassword,
          no_wa: no_wa.trim(),
          role: validRole,
          jenis_kendaraan: cleanJenisKendaraan,
          plat_nomor: cleanPlatNomor,
          kapasitas_penumpang: cleanKapasitas,
          is_online: false,
        },
      ])
      .select(USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
      .single();

    if (insertError) {
      // 23505 = unique_violation (dua registrasi bersamaan dengan NIM sama)
      if (insertError.code === '23505') {
        return res.status(409).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'NIM sudah terdaftar. Silakan login.',
        });
      }
      throw insertError;
    }

    const token = generateToken(newUser);

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Registrasi akun berhasil!',
      token,
      user: newUser,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan saat registrasi.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// POST /api/auth/login
export const login = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email, password } = req.body;

    if (!email || !password) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email dan password wajib diisi.',
      });
    }

    if (typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Format email atau password tidak valid.',
      });
    }

    const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

    const { data: user, error } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .select('*')
      .eq('email', normalizedEmail)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email atau password salah.',
      });
    }

    const isPasswordValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);

    if (!isPasswordValid) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Email atau password salah.',
      });
    }

    const { password: _, ...userWithoutPassword } = user;
    const token = generateToken(userWithoutPassword);

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Login berhasil!',
      token,
      user: userWithoutPassword,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terjadi kesalahan saat login.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// GET /api/auth/me
export const getMe = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { data: user, error } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .select(USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
      .eq('id', req.user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Data pengguna tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      user,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengambil profil pengguna.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// DELETE /api/auth/me
export const deleteMe = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { nim } = req.body || {};

    const { data: user, error: findUserError } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .select('id, nim, email')
      .eq('id', req.user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (findUserError) throw findUserError;

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Akun pengguna tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    const expected = (user.nim || user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const provided = String(nim || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!expected || provided !== expected) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Konfirmasi NIM/Email tidak sesuai dengan data akun.',
      });
    }

    // Pastikan tidak ada order aktif yang terikat pada akun ini
    const { data: activeOrders, error: activeError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id')
      .or(`customer_id.eq.${req.user.id},driver_id.eq.${req.user.id}`)
      .in('status', ACTIVE_ORDER_STATUSES)
      .limit(1);

    if (activeError) throw activeError;

    if (activeOrders && activeOrders.length > 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          'Tidak dapat menghapus akun karena masih ada pesanan yang sedang berjalan.',
      });
    }

    const { error: deleteError } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', req.user.id);

    if (deleteError) throw deleteError;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Akun berhasil dihapus secara permanen.',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menghapus akun.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};


==================================================
FILE: backend\controllers\driverController.js

import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';

const USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS =
  'id, nama, nim, email, no_wa, role, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang, is_online, created_at';

// PATCH /api/driver/status
export const updateDriverStatus = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (req.user.role !== 'driver') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya akun Driver yang dapat mengubah status online.',
      });
    }

    const { is_online } = req.body;
    if (typeof is_online !== 'boolean') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Field is_online harus berupa boolean (true/false).',
      });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .update({ is_online })
      .eq('id', req.user.id)
      .select(USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: is_online ? 'Status driver aktif (ON).' : 'Status driver nonaktif (OFF).',
      user: data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengubah status driver.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// PATCH /api/driver/vehicle
export const updateDriverVehicle = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (req.user.role !== 'driver') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya akun Driver yang dapat memperbarui data kendaraan.',
      });
    }

    const { jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang } = req.body;

    const normalizedVehicle = String(jenis_kendaraan || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!['motor', 'mobil'].includes(normalizedVehicle)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Jenis kendaraan harus berupa "motor" atau "mobil".',
      });
    }

    const cleanPlat = String(plat_nomor || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!cleanPlat) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Plat nomor wajib diisi.',
      });
    }

    let cleanKapasitas = 1;
    if (normalizedVehicle === 'mobil') {
      const parsed = Number(kapasitas_penumpang);
      if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed < 1 || parsed > 8) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Kapasitas penumpang mobil harus antara 1 hingga 8 orang.',
        });
      }
      cleanKapasitas = parsed;
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('users')
      .update({
        jenis_kendaraan: normalizedVehicle,
        plat_nomor: cleanPlat,
        kapasitas_penumpang: cleanKapasitas,
      })
      .eq('id', req.user.id)
      .select(USER_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Data kendaraan driver berhasil diperbarui.',
      user: data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memperbarui data kendaraan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};





==================================================
FILE: backend\controllers\offerController.js
==================================================
import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';
import { checkDriverEligibility } from '../utils/driverEligibility.js';

const OFFER_SELECT_QUERY = `
  *,
  driver:users!driver_id(id, nama, nim, no_wa, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang, is_online)
`;

const ORDER_SELECT_QUERY = `
  *,
  customer:users!customer_id(id, nama, nim, no_wa),
  driver:users!driver_id(id, nama, nim, no_wa, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang, is_online)
`;

const MIN_PRICE = 2000;
const MAX_PRICE = 1000000;
const MAX_PENUMPANG = 8;
const MAX_NOTE_LENGTH = 200;

const ORDER_FOR_OFFER_FIELDS =
  'id, customer_id, status, jenis_layanan, jenis_kendaraan, jumlah_penumpang';

// Kontak driver baru terlihat setelah tawaran disepakati
// (sama seperti kontak pemesan yang disembunyikan dari driver sebelum order diterima)
const sanitizeOffer = (offer) => {
  if (!offer || !offer.driver || offer.status === 'accepted') return offer;
  return {
    ...offer,
    driver: { ...offer.driver, nim: null, no_wa: null },
  };
};

// GET /api/orders/:id/offers
export const getOrderOffers = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;

    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id, customer_id, driver_id, status')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (orderError) throw orderError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (
      req.user.role === 'customer' &&
      order.customer_id !== req.user.id
    ) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Anda tidak berhak melihat tawaran untuk pesanan ini.',
      });
    }

    let query = supabase
      .from('offers')
      .select(OFFER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .eq('order_id', id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (req.user.role === 'driver') {
      query = query.eq('driver_id', req.user.id);
    }

    const { data, error } = await query;

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: (data || []).map(sanitizeOffer),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengambil daftar tawaran.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// POST /api/orders/:id/offers
export const createOrderOffer = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const { offered_price, driver_id, adjusted_passengers, note } = req.body;

    const parsedPrice = Number(offered_price);
    if (
      !Number.isInteger(parsedPrice) ||
      parsedPrice < MIN_PRICE ||
      parsedPrice > MAX_PRICE
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Nominal tawaran harus berupa angka bulat antara Rp ${MIN_PRICE.toLocaleString('id-ID')} dan Rp ${MAX_PRICE.toLocaleString('id-ID')}.`,
      });
    }

    const cleanNote = note ? String(note).trim() : '';
    if (cleanNote.length > MAX_NOTE_LENGTH) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Catatan maksimal ${MAX_NOTE_LENGTH} karakter.`,
      });
    }

    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select(ORDER_FOR_OFFER_FIELDS)
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (orderError) throw orderError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (order.status !== 'pending') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Negosiasi hanya dapat dilakukan pada pesanan yang masih menunggu driver.',
      });
    }

    const isDriver = req.user.role === 'driver';
    const offeredBy = isDriver ? 'driver' : 'customer';
    let targetDriverId = null;
    let cleanPassengers = null;

    // Penyesuaian jumlah penumpang hanya boleh dari pemesan, dan hanya untuk AnJem
    if (
      !isDriver &&
      order.jenis_layanan === 'anjem' &&
      adjusted_passengers !== undefined &&
      adjusted_passengers !== null &&
      adjusted_passengers !== ''
    ) {
      const parsedPax = Number(adjusted_passengers);
      if (
        !Number.isInteger(parsedPax) ||
        parsedPax < 1 ||
        parsedPax > MAX_PENUMPANG
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: `Jumlah penumpang harus antara 1 hingga ${MAX_PENUMPANG} orang.`,
        });
      }
      cleanPassengers = parsedPax;
    }

    if (isDriver) {
      targetDriverId = req.user.id;

      // Driver wajib ON, punya data kendaraan, dan kendaraannya cocok.
      // Kapasitas & trip aktif baru dicek saat tawaran disepakati.
      const eligibility = await checkDriverEligibility(targetDriverId, order, {
        skipCapacity: true,
        skipActiveTrip: true,
      });
      if (!eligibility.ok) {
        return res.status(eligibility.status).json({
          success: false,
          message: eligibility.message,
        });
      }
    } else {
      if (order.customer_id !== req.user.id) {
        return res.status(403).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Anda bukan pembuat pesanan ini.',
        });
      }

      targetDriverId = driver_id;
      if (!targetDriverId || typeof targetDriverId !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'driver_id wajib disertakan saat pemesan menawar balik.',
        });
      }

      // Pemesan hanya boleh menawar balik ke driver yang sudah menawar lebih dulu
      const { data: driverOffer, error: driverOfferError } = await supabase
        .from('offers')
        .select('id')
        .eq('order_id', id)
        .eq('driver_id', targetDriverId)
        .eq('offered_by', 'driver')
        .limit(1);

      if (driverOfferError) throw driverOfferError;

      if (!driverOffer || driverOffer.length === 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Driver ini belum mengirim tawaran untuk pesananmu.',
        });
      }

      const eligibility = await checkDriverEligibility(targetDriverId, order, {
        passengers: cleanPassengers ?? order.jumlah_penumpang,
        skipActiveTrip: true,
      });
      if (!eligibility.ok) {
        return res.status(eligibility.status).json({
          success: false,
          message: eligibility.message,
        });
      }
    }

    const { data: createdOffer, error: insertError } = await supabase
      .from('offers')
      .insert([
        {
          order_id: id,
          driver_id: targetDriverId,
          offered_by: offeredBy,
          offered_price: parsedPrice,
          adjusted_passengers: cleanPassengers,
          note: cleanNote || null,
          status: 'pending',
        },
      ])
      .select(OFFER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .single();

    if (insertError) throw insertError;

    // Tandai tawaran pending sebelumnya (selain yang baru) sebagai superseded
    await supabase
      .from('offers')
      .update({
        status: 'superseded',
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
      .eq('order_id', id)
      .eq('driver_id', targetDriverId)
      .eq('status', 'pending')
      .neq('id', createdOffer.id);

    // Catatan: tabel orders TIDAK diubah saat tawar-menawar.
    // Harga & jumlah penumpang final baru diterapkan ketika tawaran disepakati (accept).

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Penawaran harga berhasil dikirim.',
      data: sanitizeOffer(createdOffer),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengirim penawaran harga.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// PATCH /api/orders/:id/offers/:offerId/accept
export const acceptOrderOffer = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id, offerId } = req.params;

    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select(ORDER_FOR_OFFER_FIELDS)
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (orderError) throw orderError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (order.status !== 'pending') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan ini sudah diproses atau tidak lagi tersedia.',
      });
    }

    const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase
      .from('offers')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', offerId)
      .eq('order_id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (offerError) throw offerError;

    if (!offer) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tawaran tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (offer.status !== 'pending') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tawaran ini sudah tidak berlaku (sudah diproses atau digantikan).',
      });
    }

    // Validasi: pihak yang menyetujui harus lawan dari pembuat tawaran
    if (offer.offered_by === 'driver' && order.customer_id !== req.user.id) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya pemesan yang dapat menyetujui tawaran driver.',
      });
    }

    if (offer.offered_by === 'customer' && offer.driver_id !== req.user.id) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya driver terkait yang dapat menyetujui tawaran pemesan.',
      });
    }

    const finalPassengers =
      order.jenis_layanan === 'anjem'
        ? offer.adjusted_passengers || order.jumlah_penumpang
        : order.jumlah_penumpang;

    // Driver harus masih memenuhi syarat: ON, kendaraan cocok & muat, tidak ada trip aktif
    const eligibility = await checkDriverEligibility(offer.driver_id, order, {
      passengers: finalPassengers,
    });
    if (!eligibility.ok) {
      const isDriverSelf = req.user.id === offer.driver_id;
      return res.status(eligibility.status).json({
        success: false,
        message: isDriverSelf
          ? eligibility.message
          : 'Driver ini sedang tidak dapat menerima order (offline, kendaraan tidak sesuai, atau sedang menjalankan trip lain). Coba tawaran lain.',
      });
    }

    // Proses penerimaan dijalankan dalam satu transaksi PostgreSQL melalui RPC.
    // RPC mengunci baris order dan memperbarui order + seluruh status offer secara atomik.
    const { data: acceptance, error: acceptanceError } = await supabase.rpc(
      'accept_order_offer_atomic',
      {
        p_order_id: id,
        p_offer_id: offerId,
        p_actor_id: req.user.id,
      }
    );

    if (acceptanceError) throw acceptanceError;

    if (!acceptance?.success) {
      return res.status(acceptance?.status || 409).json({
        success: false,
        message: acceptance?.message || 'Pesanan atau tawaran sudah berubah. Muat ulang lalu coba lagi.',
      });
    }

    // Ambil ulang relasi customer/driver agar bentuk respons tetap sama seperti API sebelumnya.
    const { data: updatedOrder, error: fetchUpdatedOrderError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select(ORDER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .eq('id', id)
      .single();

    if (fetchUpdatedOrderError) throw fetchUpdatedOrderError;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Tawaran disepakati! Pesanan kini aktif.',
      data: updatedOrder,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menyetujui tawaran.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// PATCH /api/orders/:id/offers/:offerId/reject
export const rejectOrderOffer = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id, offerId } = req.params;

    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id, customer_id, status')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (orderError) throw orderError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (order.status !== 'pending') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan ini sudah diproses atau tidak lagi tersedia.',
      });
    }

    const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase
      .from('offers')
      .select('id, order_id, driver_id, offered_by, status')
      .eq('id', offerId)
      .eq('order_id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (offerError) throw offerError;

    if (!offer) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tawaran tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (offer.status !== 'pending') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tawaran ini sudah tidak berlaku (sudah diproses atau digantikan).',
      });
    }

    // Yang boleh menolak adalah lawan dari pembuat tawaran
    const isAllowed =
      (offer.offered_by === 'driver' && order.customer_id === req.user.id) ||
      (offer.offered_by === 'customer' && offer.driver_id === req.user.id);

    if (!isAllowed) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Anda tidak berhak menolak tawaran ini.',
      });
    }

    const { data: rejected, error: rejectError } = await supabase
      .from('offers')
      .update({ status: 'rejected', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', offerId)
      .eq('status', 'pending')
      .select(OFFER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (rejectError) throw rejectError;

    if (!rejected) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Tawaran ini baru saja berubah. Muat ulang lalu coba lagi.',
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Tawaran ditolak.',
      data: sanitizeOffer(rejected),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menolak tawaran.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};



==================================================
FILE: backend\controllers\orderController.js
==================================================
import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';
import { checkDriverEligibility } from '../utils/driverEligibility.js';

const ORDER_SELECT_QUERY = `
  *,
  customer:users!customer_id(id, nama, nim, no_wa),
  driver:users!driver_id(id, nama, nim, no_wa, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang, is_online)
`;

const MAX_ACTIVE_ORDERS_PER_CUSTOMER = 3;
const PENDING_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 30;
const JADWAL_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 30;
const MAX_PENUMPANG = 8;
const MIN_ONGKIR = 2000;
const MAX_ONGKIR = 1000000;
const ACTIVE_STATUSES = ['pending', 'accepted', 'on_progress'];

const hasValue = (v) => v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v).trim() !== '';

// Driver tidak boleh melihat kontak pemesan sebelum order diterima
const hideCustomerContact = (order, user) => {
  if (user.role === 'driver' && order.status === 'pending' && order.customer) {
    return {
      ...order,
      customer: { ...order.customer, nim: null, no_wa: null },
    };
  }
  return order;
};

// POST /api/orders (Customer membuat pesanan baru)
export const createOrder = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (req.user.role !== 'customer') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya akun dengan role Customer yang dapat membuat pesanan.',
      });
    }

    const {
      jenis_layanan,
      titik_jemput,
      titik_antar,
      catatan,
      ongkir,
      jenis_kendaraan,
      jumlah_penumpang,
      waktu_berangkat,
      jadwal_at,
      nama_barang,
      perkiraan_harga_barang,
    } = req.body;

    if (
      !jenis_layanan ||
      typeof titik_jemput !== 'string' ||
      typeof titik_antar !== 'string' ||
      titik_jemput.trim() === '' ||
      titik_antar.trim() === ''
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Jenis layanan, titik jemput, dan titik antar wajib diisi.',
      });
    }

    if (!['anjem', 'jastip'].includes(jenis_layanan)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Jenis layanan harus berupa "anjem" atau "jastip".',
      });
    }

    if (titik_jemput.trim().length > 200 || titik_antar.trim().length > 200) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Titik jemput/antar maksimal 200 karakter.',
      });
    }

    if (typeof catatan === 'string' && catatan.trim().length > 500) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Catatan maksimal 500 karakter.',
      });
    }

    // Jenis kendaraan (opsional)
    let cleanJenisKendaraan = null;
    if (hasValue(jenis_kendaraan)) {
      const normalizedVehicle = String(jenis_kendaraan).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!['motor', 'mobil'].includes(normalizedVehicle)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Jenis kendaraan harus berupa "motor" atau "mobil".',
        });
      }
      cleanJenisKendaraan = normalizedVehicle;
    }

    // Jumlah penumpang: hanya relevan untuk AnJem (JasTip tidak mengirimnya)
    let cleanJumlahPenumpang = null;
    if (jenis_layanan === 'anjem') {
      if (hasValue(jumlah_penumpang)) {
        const parsedPenumpang = Number(jumlah_penumpang);
        if (!Number.isInteger(parsedPenumpang) || parsedPenumpang < 1) {
          return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: 'Jumlah penumpang harus berupa angka minimal 1.',
          });
        }
        cleanJumlahPenumpang = parsedPenumpang;
      } else {
        cleanJumlahPenumpang = 1;
      }

      if (cleanJenisKendaraan === 'motor' && cleanJumlahPenumpang > 1) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Motor hanya dapat mengangkut 1 penumpang.',
        });
      }

      if (cleanJumlahPenumpang > MAX_PENUMPANG) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: `Jumlah penumpang maksimal ${MAX_PENUMPANG} orang.`,
        });
      }
    }

    // Waktu berangkat
    let cleanWaktuBerangkat = null;
    if (hasValue(waktu_berangkat)) {
      const normalizedWaktu = String(waktu_berangkat).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!['sekarang', 'jadwal'].includes(normalizedWaktu)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Waktu berangkat harus berupa "sekarang" atau "jadwal".',
        });
      }
      cleanWaktuBerangkat = normalizedWaktu;
    }

    let cleanJadwalAt = null;
    if (hasValue(jadwal_at)) {
      const parsedDate = Date.parse(jadwal_at);
      if (Number.isNaN(parsedDate)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Format waktu jadwal_at tidak valid.',
        });
      }
      cleanJadwalAt = new Date(parsedDate).toISOString();
    }

    if (cleanWaktuBerangkat === 'jadwal') {
      if (!cleanJadwalAt || Date.parse(cleanJadwalAt) < Date.now() - 60 * 1000) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Waktu jadwal harus diisi dan tidak boleh di masa lalu.',
        });
      }
    }

    const cleanNamaBarang = hasValue(nama_barang) ? String(nama_barang).trim() : null;

    let cleanPerkiraanHargaBarang = null;
    if (hasValue(perkiraan_harga_barang)) {
      const parsedHargaBarang = Number(perkiraan_harga_barang);
      if (Number.isNaN(parsedHargaBarang) || parsedHargaBarang < 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Perkiraan harga barang tidak valid.',
        });
      }
      cleanPerkiraanHargaBarang = parsedHargaBarang;
    }

    // Ongkir wajib valid (tidak lagi diam-diam jadi 5000)
    const parsedOngkir = Number(ongkir);
    if (
      !Number.isInteger(parsedOngkir) ||
      parsedOngkir < MIN_ONGKIR ||
      parsedOngkir > MAX_ONGKIR
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Ongkir harus berupa angka bulat antara Rp ${MIN_ONGKIR.toLocaleString('id-ID')} dan Rp ${MAX_ONGKIR.toLocaleString('id-ID')}.`,
      });
    }

    // Batasi jumlah order aktif per pemesan (anti spam)
    const { count: activeCount, error: countError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('customer_id', req.user.id)
      .in('status', ACTIVE_STATUSES);

    if (countError) throw countError;

    if ((activeCount || 0) >= MAX_ACTIVE_ORDERS_PER_CUSTOMER) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Maksimal ${MAX_ACTIVE_ORDERS_PER_CUSTOMER} pesanan aktif sekaligus. Selesaikan atau batalkan pesanan lain dulu.`,
      });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .insert([
        {
          customer_id: req.user.id,
          jenis_layanan,
          titik_jemput: titik_jemput.trim(),
          titik_antar: titik_antar.trim(),
          catatan: typeof catatan === 'string' ? catatan.trim() : '',
          ongkir: parsedOngkir,
          status: 'pending',
          jenis_kendaraan: cleanJenisKendaraan,
          jumlah_penumpang: cleanJumlahPenumpang,
          waktu_berangkat: cleanWaktuBerangkat,
          jadwal_at: cleanJadwalAt,
          nama_barang: cleanNamaBarang,
          perkiraan_harga_barang: cleanPerkiraanHargaBarang,
        },
      ])
      .select(ORDER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Pesanan berhasil dibuat! Menunggu driver mengambil order.',
      data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal membuat pesanan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// GET /api/orders (Customer melihat pesanannya; Driver melihat order pending + order miliknya)
export const getOrders = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    // Order pending "Sekarang" yang tidak diambil siapa pun otomatis kedaluwarsa
    try {
      const cutoff = new Date(
        Date.now() - PENDING_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60 * 1000
      ).toISOString();
      await supabase
        .from('orders')
        .update({ status: 'cancelled', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
        .eq('status', 'pending')
        .lt('created_at', cutoff)
        .or('waktu_berangkat.is.null,waktu_berangkat.neq.jadwal');

      // Order "Jadwal" kedaluwarsa bila waktunya sudah lewat > 30 menit dan belum diambil
      const jadwalCutoff = new Date(
        Date.now() - JADWAL_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60 * 1000
      ).toISOString();
      await supabase
        .from('orders')
        .update({ status: 'cancelled', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
        .eq('status', 'pending')
        .eq('waktu_berangkat', 'jadwal')
        .lt('jadwal_at', jadwalCutoff);
    } catch {
      // Kegagalan housekeeping tidak boleh menggagalkan daftar order
    }

    let query = supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select(ORDER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (req.user.role === 'customer') {
      query = query.eq('customer_id', req.user.id);
    } else if (req.user.role === 'driver') {
      query = query.or(`status.eq.pending,driver_id.eq.${req.user.id}`);
    }

    const { data, error } = await query;

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: (data || []).map((order) => hideCustomerContact(order, req.user)),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengambil daftar pesanan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// PATCH /api/orders/:id/accept (Driver mengambil pesanan pending)
export const acceptOrder = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (req.user.role !== 'driver') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Hanya akun dengan role Driver yang dapat mengambil pesanan.',
      });
    }

    const { id } = req.params;

    const { data: order, error: findError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id, status, driver_id, jenis_layanan, jenis_kendaraan, jumlah_penumpang')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (findError) throw findError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (order.status !== 'pending' || order.driver_id) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan ini sudah diambil oleh driver lain atau tidak lagi tersedia.',
      });
    }

    // Validasi: online, kendaraan lengkap & cocok, kapasitas cukup, tidak ada trip aktif
    const eligibility = await checkDriverEligibility(req.user.id, order);
    if (!eligibility.ok) {
      return res.status(eligibility.status).json({
        success: false,
        message: eligibility.message,
      });
    }

    // Update bersyarat untuk mencegah race condition antar driver
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .update({
        driver_id: req.user.id,
        status: 'accepted',
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
      .eq('id', id)
      .eq('status', 'pending')
      .is('driver_id', null)
      .select(ORDER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;

    if (!data) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan ini sudah diambil oleh driver lain atau tidak lagi tersedia.',
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Pesanan berhasil diambil! Segera hubungi customer via WhatsApp.',
      data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengambil pesanan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// PATCH /api/orders/:id/status (Update status perjalanan / pembatalan)
export const updateOrderStatus = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const { status } = req.body;

    const allowedStatuses = ['on_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'];
    if (!allowedStatuses.includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Status tidak valid. Pilihan: on_progress, completed, cancelled.',
      });
    }

    const { data: order, error: findError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (findError) throw findError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS = {
      accepted: ['on_progress', 'cancelled'],
      on_progress: ['completed', 'cancelled'],
      pending: ['cancelled'],
    };

    if (!(ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[order.status] || []).includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Pesanan berstatus "${order.status}" tidak dapat diubah menjadi "${status}".`,
      });
    }

    if (status === 'cancelled') {
      // Pemesan boleh membatalkan selama belum mulai diantar (pending / accepted)
      const isOwnerCustomer =
        order.customer_id === req.user.id &&
        ['pending', 'accepted'].includes(order.status);
      const isAssignedDriver = order.driver_id === req.user.id;

      if (!isOwnerCustomer && !isAssignedDriver) {
        return res.status(403).json({
          success: false,
          message: 'Pesanan tidak dapat dibatalkan pada tahap ini.',
        });
      }
    } else if (order.driver_id !== req.user.id) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Anda bukan driver yang bertugas untuk pesanan ini.',
      });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .update({
        status,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
      .eq('id', id)
      .eq('status', order.status)
      .select(ORDER_SELECT_QUERY)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;

    if (!data) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Status pesanan baru saja berubah. Muat ulang lalu coba lagi.',
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: `Status pesanan diperbarui menjadi "${status}".`,
      data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal memperbarui status pesanan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

// DELETE /api/orders/:id (Customer menghapus riwayat pesanan yang pending/cancelled)
export const deleteOrder = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;

    const { data: order, error: findError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id, customer_id, status')
      .eq('id', id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (findError) throw findError;

    if (!order) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.',
      });
    }

    if (order.customer_id !== req.user.id) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Anda tidak berhak menghapus pesanan ini.',
      });
    }

    if (!['pending', 'cancelled'].includes(order.status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Pesanan yang sedang berjalan atau sudah selesai tidak dapat dihapus.',
      });
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.from('orders').delete().eq('id', id);

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Pesanan berhasil dihapus.',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal menghapus pesanan.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};

import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';

// GET /api/spots
export const getAllSpots = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { kategori } = req.query;

    let query = supabase
      .from('spots')
      .select('*')
      .order('kategori', { ascending: true })
      .order('nama_lokasi', { ascending: true });

    if (kategori) {
      query = query.eq('kategori', kategori);
    }

    const { data, error } = await query;

    if (error) throw error;

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Gagal mengambil data lokasi spots.',
      error: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? undefined : error.message,
    });
  }
};




==================================================
FILE: backend\middleware\authMiddleware.js
==================================================
import 'dotenv/config';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';

export default function authMiddleware(req, res, next) {
  const header = req.headers.authorization || '';
  const [scheme, token] = header.split(' ');

  if (scheme !== 'Bearer' || !token) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Akses ditolak. Token autentikasi tidak ditemukan.',
    });
  }

  try {
    req.user = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    const expired = error.name === 'TokenExpiredError';
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: expired
        ? 'Sesi telah berakhir. Silakan login kembali.'
        : 'Token tidak valid.',
    });
  }
}


==================================================
FILE: backend\middleware\rateLimit.js
==================================================
const WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 20;
const attempts = new Map();

export default function loginRateLimit(req, res, next) {
  const key = req.ip || 'unknown';
  const now = Date.now();
  const record = attempts.get(key);

  if (!record || now > record.resetAt) {
    attempts.set(key, { count: 1, resetAt: now + WINDOW_MS });
    return next();
  }

  record.count += 1;
  if (record.count > MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
    return res.status(429).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Terlalu banyak percobaan. Coba lagi dalam beberapa menit.',
    });
  }
  return next();
}

==================================================
FILE: backend\utils\driverEligibility.js
==================================================
import supabase from '../config/supabase.js';

const BUSY_STATUSES = ['accepted', 'on_progress'];

const fail = (status, message) => ({ ok: false, status, message });

/**
 * Cek apakah driver boleh mengambil/menawar sebuah order.
 * options:
 *  - passengers: jumlah penumpang final (default: order.jumlah_penumpang)
 *  - skipCapacity: lewati cek kapasitas (dipakai saat driver baru menawar)
 *  - skipActiveTrip: lewati cek trip aktif (dipakai saat driver baru menawar)
 */
export async function checkDriverEligibility(driverId, order, options = {}) {
  const { passengers, skipCapacity = false, skipActiveTrip = false } = options;

  const { data: driver, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('id, role, is_online, jenis_kendaraan, plat_nomor, kapasitas_penumpang')
    .eq('id', driverId)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error) throw error;

  if (!driver || driver.role !== 'driver') {
    return fail(404, 'Driver tidak ditemukan.');
  }

  if (!driver.is_online) {
    return fail(400, 'Aktifkan status ON terlebih dahulu sebelum menerima atau menawar order.');
  }

  if (!driver.jenis_kendaraan || !driver.plat_nomor) {
    return fail(400, 'Lengkapi data kendaraan di halaman Profil sebelum menerima order.');
  }

  if (order.jenis_layanan === 'anjem') {
    if (order.jenis_kendaraan && order.jenis_kendaraan !== driver.jenis_kendaraan) {
      return fail(
        400,
        `Order ini meminta ${order.jenis_kendaraan}, sedangkan kendaraanmu ${driver.jenis_kendaraan}.`
      );
    }

    if (!skipCapacity) {
      const capacity =
        driver.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil'
          ? Number(driver.kapasitas_penumpang) || 4
          : 1;
      const pax = Number(passengers ?? order.jumlah_penumpang) || 1;
      if (pax > capacity) {
        return fail(
          400,
          `Jumlah penumpang (${pax}) melebihi kapasitas kendaraan (${capacity}).`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  if (!skipActiveTrip) {
    const { data: busy, error: busyError } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id')
      .eq('driver_id', driverId)
      .in('status', BUSY_STATUSES)
      .limit(1);

    if (busyError) throw busyError;

    if (busy && busy.length > 0) {
      return fail(400, 'Selesaikan pesanan yang sedang berjalan sebelum mengambil order baru.');
    }
  }

  return { ok: true, driver };
}

# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.

FILE: frontend\src\App.jsx

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './context/AuthContext';
import ProtectedRoute from './components/ProtectedRoute';
import LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage';
import RegisterPage from './pages/RegisterPage';
import CustomerBeranda from './pages/customer/CustomerBeranda';
import CustomerAnjem from './pages/customer/CustomerAnjem';
import CustomerJastip from './pages/customer/CustomerJastip';
import CustomerMencari from './pages/customer/CustomerMencari';
import CustomerTawaran from './pages/customer/CustomerTawaran';
import CustomerPesanan from './pages/customer/CustomerPesanan';
import CustomerLacak from './pages/customer/CustomerLacak';
import CustomerHistory from './pages/customer/CustomerHistory';
import CustomerProfil from './pages/customer/CustomerProfil';
import CustomerHapusAkun from './pages/customer/CustomerHapusAkun';
import DriverBeranda from './pages/driver/DriverBeranda';
import DriverRequestDetail from './pages/driver/DriverRequestDetail';
import DriverNego from './pages/driver/DriverNego';
import DriverPesanan from './pages/driver/DriverPesanan';
import DriverNavigasi from './pages/driver/DriverNavigasi';
import DriverHistory from './pages/driver/DriverHistory';
import DriverProfil from './pages/driver/DriverProfil';
import DriverHapusAkun from './pages/driver/DriverHapusAkun';

function RootRedirect() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-100">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Memuat...</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  return <Navigate to={user.role === 'driver' ? '/driver' : '/customer'} replace />;
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<RootRedirect />} />
          <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
          <Route path="/register" element={<RegisterPage />} />

          {/* Rute Pemesan (KELOMPOK A) */}
          <Route
            path="/customer"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerBeranda />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/anjem"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerAnjem />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/jastip"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerJastip />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/mencari"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerMencari />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/tawaran"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerTawaran />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/pesanan"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerPesanan />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/lacak"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerLacak />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/history"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerHistory />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/profil"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerProfil />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/hapus-akun"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <CustomerHapusAkun />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/customer/*"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="customer">
                <Navigate to="/customer" replace />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />

          {/* Rute Driver (KELOMPOK B) */}
          <Route
            path="/driver"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverBeranda />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/request/:id"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverRequestDetail />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/nego/:id"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverNego />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/pesanan"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverPesanan />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/navigasi"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverNavigasi />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/history"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverHistory />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/profil"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverProfil />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/hapus-akun"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <DriverHapusAkun />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/driver/*"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute allowedRole="driver">
                <Navigate to="/driver" replace />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />

          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
        </Routes>
      </BrowserRouter>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\api\axios.js

import axios from 'axios';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'anjemdips_token';

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000/api',
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
  timeout: 15000,
});

// Otomatis sisipkan token JWT dari localStorage pada setiap request
api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    if (token) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

// Token kedaluwarsa/tidak valid -> bersihkan sesi dan arahkan ke halaman login.
// Request login/register dikecualikan agar pesan "password salah" tetap tampil.
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    const url = error.config?.url || '';
    const isAuthForm = url.includes('/auth/login') || url.includes('/auth/register');
    if (
      error.response?.status === 401 &&
      !isAuthForm &&
      localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)
    ) {
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
      if (window.location.pathname !== '/login') {
        window.location.replace('/login');
      }
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;

  return (

      aria-label="Navigasi utama"

        right: 'auto',
        bottom: 0,
        transform: 'translateX(-50%)',

        height: '68px',
        background: '#fff',
        boxShadow: '0 -1px 0 #E8EBEF',
        display: 'flex',
        padding: '0 8px calc(6px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))',
        marginTop: 0,
        zIndex: 20,
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}
    >
      {items.map((item) => (
        <NavLink
          key={item.to}
          to={item.to}
          end={item.end}
          style={({ isActive }) => ({
            flex: 1,
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',

            justifyContent: 'center',
            gap: '3px',
            fontSize: '10px',
            fontWeight: 600,

            textDecoration: 'none',
          })}
        >
          {item.icon}
          <span>{item.label}</span>
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const BUTTON_VARIANTS = {
  primary: {
    background: '#0F766E',
    color: '#fff',
    boxShadow: 'none',
  },
  amber: {
    background: '#F59E0B',
    color: '#3B2402',
    boxShadow: 'none',
  },
  outline: {
    background: '#fff',
    color: '#0F172A',
    boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
  },
  danger: {
    background: '#B91C1C',
    color: '#fff',
    boxShadow: 'none',
  },
  dangerOutline: {
    background: '#fff',
    color: '#B91C1C',
    boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
  },
  disabled: {
    background: '#E8EAEE',
    color: '#64748B',
    boxShadow: 'none',
  },
};

export default function Button({
  children,
  to,
  variant = 'primary',
  height = 48,
  disabled = false,
  type = 'button',
  style,
  className = '',
  ...props
}) {
  const activeVariant = disabled ? 'disabled' : variant;
  const variantStyle = BUTTON_VARIANTS[activeVariant] || BUTTON_VARIANTS.primary;

  const baseStyle = {
    height: `${height}px`,
    borderRadius: '14px',
    border: 0,
    font: "700 14px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
    display: 'flex',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: '8px',
    cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
    width: '100%',
    textDecoration: 'none',
    boxSizing: 'border-box',
    ...variantStyle,
    ...style,
  };

  if (to && !disabled) {
    return (
      <Link to={to} className={className} style={baseStyle} {...props}>
        {children}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={className}
      style={baseStyle}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const VARIANT_STYLES = {
  default: {
    background: '#fff',
    borderRadius: '18px',
    padding: '14px',
    boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,.05),0 0 0 1px rgba(15,23,42,.04)',
  },
  soft: {
    background: '#F0F7F6',
    borderRadius: '18px',
    padding: '14px',
    boxShadow: 'none',
  },
  danger: {
    background: '#FFF6F6',
    borderRadius: '18px',
    padding: '14px',
    boxShadow: '0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
  },
};

export default function Card({
  children,
  to,
  variant = 'default',
  style,
  className = '',
  ...props
}) {
  const mergedStyle = {
    ...VARIANT_STYLES[variant],
    ...style,
  };

  if (to) {
    return (
      <Link
        to={to}
        className={className}
        style={{
          textDecoration: 'none',
          color: 'inherit',
          ...mergedStyle,
        }}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className={className} style={mergedStyle} {...props}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

const INPUT_VARIANTS = {

    padding: '0 12px',
    font: "600 13px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",

  },

export default function Input({
  label,
  hideLabel = false,
  variant = 'filled',
  containerStyle,
  labelStyle,
  style,
  ...props
}) {
  const variantStyle = INPUT_VARIANTS[variant] || INPUT_VARIANTS.filled;

  const inputElement = (
    <input
      style={{
        width: '100%',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        border: 0,
        outline: 0,
        borderRadius: '12px',
        color: '#0F172A',
        ...variantStyle,
        ...style,
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  );

  if (!label) {
    return inputElement;
  }

  return (
    <label style={{ display: 'block', ...containerStyle }}>
      <span
        style={{
          display: hideLabel ? 'none' : 'block',
          fontSize: '11px',
          fontWeight: 600,
          color: '#475569',
          marginBottom: '6px',
          ...labelStyle,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </span>
      {inputElement}
    </label>
  );
}

import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { LogOut, Bike, User, MapPin } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Navbar() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate('/login');
  };

  if (!user) return null;

  const isDriver = user.role === 'driver';

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-[#0B4F4A] px-5 py-4 text-white">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-[38px] w-[38px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[12px] bg-white">
            <MapPin className="h-[19px] w-[19px] text-[#0F766E]" strokeWidth={2.25} />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-[16px] font-extrabold leading-[1.2] tracking-[-0.02em] text-white">
                AnjemDips
              </span>
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[99px] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.02em] ${
                  isDriver
                    ? 'bg-[#DDF3E4] text-[#14532D]'
                    : 'bg-[#FBBF24] text-[#0F172A]'
                }`}
              >
                {isDriver ? (

                    <Bike className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={2.25} />
                    <span>Driver</span>

                    <span>Pemesan</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </span>

              Halo, {user.nama}
            </p>

          type="button"

          title="Keluar Akun"
          className="inline-flex shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[12px] bg-white/12 px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-white/20 active:scale-95"
        >
          <LogOut className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2.2} />
          <span>Keluar</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx

import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

export default function ProtectedRoute({ children, allowedRole }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-100">
        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 animate-pulse">
          Memuat sesi AnjemDips...
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (allowedRole && user.role !== allowedRole) {
    return <Navigate to={user.role === 'driver' ? '/driver' : '/customer'} replace />;
  }

  return children;
}

==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx
==================================================
/* eslint-disable react-refresh/only-export-components */
import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import api from '../api/axios';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'anjemdips_token';
const USER_CACHE_KEY = 'anjemdips_user';

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

function readCachedUser() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;

    const user = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!user?.id || !['customer', 'driver'].includes(user.role)) {
      return null;
    }

    return user;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// JWT dibaca hanya untuk memulihkan identitas tampilan saat offline.
// Token tetap divalidasi oleh backend setiap kali API dipanggil.
function readUserFromToken(token) {
  try {
    const payloadPart = token.split('.')[1];
    if (!payloadPart) return null;

    const base64 = payloadPart.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const padded = base64.padEnd(
      base64.length + ((4 - (base64.length % 4)) % 4),
      '='
    );
    const payload = JSON.parse(atob(padded));

    if (!payload?.id || !['customer', 'driver'].includes(payload.role)) {
      return null;
    }

    return {
      id: payload.id,
      email: payload.email || '',
      role: payload.role,
      nama: payload.nama || '',
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function saveUserCache(user) {
  if (!user?.id || !['customer', 'driver'].includes(user.role)) return;

  try {
    localStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Cache sesi tidak dapat disimpan:', error);
  }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(() => readCachedUser());
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const restoreSession = async () => {
      const token = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);

      if (!token) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setUser(null);
          setLoading(false);
        }
        return;
      }

      // Tampilkan sesi tersimpan secepatnya agar halaman History bisa dibuka
      // tanpa menunggu server. Jika cache profil belum ada, gunakan klaim JWT.
      const localUser = readCachedUser() || readUserFromToken(token);

      if (localUser && !cancelled) {
        setUser(localUser);
        saveUserCache(localUser);
        setLoading(false);
      }

      try {
        const res = await api.get('/auth/me');
        const freshUser = res.data?.user;

        if (!cancelled && freshUser?.id) {
          setUser(freshUser);
          saveUserCache(freshUser);
          setLoading(false);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        const status = err.response?.status;

        // Hanya respons autentikasi yang jelas tidak valid yang mengakhiri sesi.
        // Gangguan jaringan/server tidak boleh dianggap sebagai logout.
        if (status === 401) {
          localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
          localStorage.removeItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);

          if (!cancelled) {
            setUser(null);
            setLoading(false);
          }
          return;
        }

        if (!cancelled) {
          setUser(localUser || null);
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    restoreSession();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  const applyAuthResponse = useCallback((data) => {
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, data.token);
    saveUserCache(data.user);
    setUser(data.user);
    return data.user;
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(
    async (email, password) => {
      const res = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password });
      return applyAuthResponse(res.data);
    },
    [applyAuthResponse]
  );

  const register = useCallback(
    async (payload) => {
      const res = await api.post('/auth/register', payload);
      return applyAuthResponse(res.data);
    },
    [applyAuthResponse]
  );

  const updateUser = useCallback((patch) => {
    setUser((prev) => {
      if (!prev) return prev;

      const next = { ...prev, ...patch };
      saveUserCache(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);
    setUser(null);
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ user, loading, login, register, logout, updateUser }),
    [user, loading, login, register, logout, updateUser]
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error('useAuth harus dipakai di dalam <AuthProvider>.');
  }
  return ctx;
}

// Helper tampilan untuk driver. Status online, update kendaraan, tawaran harga,
// dan hapus akun kini memakai API backend langsung (lihat halaman terkait).

export const DRIVER_NEGO_INCREMENTS = [2000, 3000, 5000];

// Profil kendaraan diambil apa adanya dari data user (backend).
// Jika belum diisi, nilainya null dan `lengkap` bernilai false (tidak ada data palsu).
export function getDriverVehicleProfile(user) {
  const jenis = user?.jenis_kendaraan || null;
  const plat = user?.plat_nomor || null;

  let kapasitas = null;
  if (jenis === 'mobil') {
    kapasitas = Number(user?.kapasitas_penumpang) || 4;
  } else if (jenis === 'motor') {
    kapasitas = 1;
  }

  return {
    jenis_kendaraan: jenis,
    plat_nomor: plat,
    kapasitas_penumpang: kapasitas,
    lengkap: Boolean(jenis && plat),
  };
}
