import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';

function formatRibuan(digits) {
  if (!digits) return '';
  return Number(digits).toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

const MAX_PENUMPANG = { motor: 1, mobil: 8 };

export default function CustomerAnjem() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [titikJemput, setTitikJemput] = useState('');
  const [titikAntar, setTitikAntar] = useState('');
  const [jenisKendaraan, setJenisKendaraan] = useState('mobil'); // 'motor' | 'mobil'
  const [waktuBerangkat, setWaktuBerangkat] = useState('sekarang'); // 'sekarang' | 'jadwal'
  const [jadwalAt, setJadwalAt] = useState('');
  const [jumlahPenumpang, setJumlahPenumpang] = useState(3);
  const [ongkirDigits, setOngkirDigits] = useState('15000');

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const maxPenumpang = MAX_PENUMPANG[jenisKendaraan];

  const handleSelectKendaraan = (vehicle) => {
    setJenisKendaraan(vehicle);
    if (vehicle === 'motor' && jumlahPenumpang > 1) {
      setJumlahPenumpang(1);
    } else if (vehicle === 'mobil' && jumlahPenumpang === 1) {
      setJumlahPenumpang(3);
    }
  };

  const handleOngkirChange = (e) => {
    const raw = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
    setOngkirDigits(raw);
    if (error) setError('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    const cleanJemput = titikJemput.trim();
    const cleanAntar = titikAntar.trim();
    const numericOngkir = Number(ongkirDigits);

    if (!cleanJemput || !cleanAntar) {
      setError('Titik jemput dan tujuan wajib diisi.');
      return;
    }

    if (!numericOngkir || numericOngkir < 2000) {
      setError('Minimal tawaran harga adalah Rp 2.000.');
      return;
    }

    let jadwalIso = null;
    if (waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal') {
      const picked = new Date(jadwalAt);
      if (
        !jadwalAt ||
        Number.isNaN(picked.getTime()) ||
        picked.getTime() < Date.now()
      ) {
        setError(
          'Pilih tanggal dan jam berangkat yang valid (tidak di masa lalu).'
        );
        return;
      }
      jadwalIso = picked.toISOString();
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        jenis_layanan: 'anjem',
        titik_jemput: cleanJemput,
        titik_antar: cleanAntar,
        ongkir: numericOngkir,
        jenis_kendaraan: jenisKendaraan,
        jumlah_penumpang: jumlahPenumpang,
        waktu_berangkat: waktuBerangkat,
        jadwal_at: jadwalIso,
      };

      const res = await api.post('/orders', payload);
      navigate('/customer/mencari', {
        state: { order: res.data?.data },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal membuat pesanan. Coba lagi.'
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      noValidate
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to="/customer"
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '17px',
            letterSpacing: '-.01em',
          }}
        >
          Pesan AnJem
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '6px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Titik Jemput & Tujuan + Peta */}
        <Card
          style={{
            padding: '12px',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '8px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '3px',
                flex: 'none',
              }}
            >
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '10px',
                  height: '10px',
                  borderRadius: '99px',
                  background: '#0F766E',
                }}
              />
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '2px',
                  height: '22px',
                  background: '#D5DAE1',
                }}
              />
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '10px',
                  height: '10px',
                  borderRadius: '3px',
                  background: '#F59E0B',
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '8px',
              }}
            >
              <Input
                label="Titik jemput"
                hideLabel
                aria-label="Titik jemput"
                placeholder="Jemput di mana? (contoh: Kos Sirojudin)"
                value={titikJemput}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setTitikJemput(e.target.value);
                  if (error) setError('');
                }}
              />
              <Input
                label="Tujuan"
                hideLabel
                aria-label="Tujuan"
                placeholder="Mau ke mana?"
                value={titikAntar}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setTitikAntar(e.target.value);
                  if (error) setError('');
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              height: '92px',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#E9EEF0',
              position: 'relative',
              overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
          >
            <svg
              width="100%"
              height="92"
              viewBox="0 0 330 92"
              preserveAspectRatio="none"
              fill="none"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path
                d="M-5 70 C60 60 90 20 160 44 S280 80 340 20"
                stroke="#fff"
                strokeWidth="10"
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
              <path d="M110 -5 L150 100" stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="6" />
              <path
                d="M-5 70 C60 60 90 20 160 44 S280 80 340 20"
                stroke="#0F766E"
                strokeWidth="3"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeDasharray="1 7"
              />
              <circle cx="52" cy="62" r="7" fill="#0F766E" />
              <circle cx="268" cy="62" r="7" fill="#F59E0B" />
            </svg>
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Kendaraan, Berangkat & Penumpang */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#475569',
                marginBottom: '6px',
              }}
            >
              Jenis kendaraan
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                background: '#F1F3F6',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                padding: '3px',
                gap: '3px',
              }}
            >
              <div
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                aria-pressed={jenisKendaraan === 'motor'}
                onClick={() => handleSelectKendaraan('motor')}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleSelectKendaraan('motor');
                  }
                }}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '38px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '6px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: '12px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  background:
                    jenisKendaraan === 'motor' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: jenisKendaraan === 'motor' ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                  boxShadow:
                    jenisKendaraan === 'motor'
                      ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                      : 'none',
                }}
              >
                <svg
                  width="16"
                  height="16"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="2"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <circle cx="6" cy="16" r="3" />
                  <circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3" />
                  <path d="M6 16l4-7h5l3 7M9 9H7" />
                </svg>
                Motor
              </div>

              <div
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                aria-pressed={jenisKendaraan === 'mobil'}
                onClick={() => handleSelectKendaraan('mobil')}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleSelectKendaraan('mobil');
                  }
                }}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '38px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: '6px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: '12px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  background:
                    jenisKendaraan === 'mobil' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: jenisKendaraan === 'mobil' ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                  boxShadow:
                    jenisKendaraan === 'mobil'
                      ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                      : 'none',
                }}
              >
                <svg
                  width="16"
                  height="16"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="2"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <path d="M4 16v-4l2-5h12l2 5v4zM4 16v3M20 16v3" />
                </svg>
                Mobil
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px' }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: '11px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#475569',
                  marginBottom: '6px',
                }}
              >
                Berangkat
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  background: '#F1F3F6',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  padding: '3px',
                  gap: '3px',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  aria-pressed={waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang'}
                  onClick={() => setWaktuBerangkat('sekarang')}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      setWaktuBerangkat('sekarang');
                    }
                  }}
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '9px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    gap: '6px',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    fontSize: '12px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    background:
                      waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                    color:
                      waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang' ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                    boxShadow:
                      waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang'
                        ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                        : 'none',
                  }}
                >
                  Sekarang
                </div>
                <div
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  aria-pressed={waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal'}
                  onClick={() => setWaktuBerangkat('jadwal')}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      setWaktuBerangkat('jadwal');
                    }
                  }}
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    height: '38px',
                    borderRadius: '9px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    gap: '6px',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    fontSize: '12px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    background:
                      waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                    color: waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                    boxShadow:
                      waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal'
                        ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                        : 'none',
                  }}
                >
                  Jadwal
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: '11px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#475569',
                  marginBottom: '6px',
                }}
              >
                Jumlah penumpang
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  height: '44px',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  background: '#F5F6F8',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  padding: '0 5px',
                }}
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Kurangi"
                  disabled={jumlahPenumpang <= 1}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setJumlahPenumpang((prev) => Math.max(1, prev - 1))
                  }
                  style={{
                    opacity: jumlahPenumpang <= 1 ? 0.4 : 1,
                    width: '34px',
                    height: '34px',
                    borderRadius: '10px',
                    border: 0,
                    background: '#fff',
                    fontSize: '18px',
                    color: '#0F172A',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,.1)',
                  }}
                >
                  -
                </button>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
                  {jumlahPenumpang}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Tambah"
                  disabled={jumlahPenumpang >= maxPenumpang}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setJumlahPenumpang((prev) => Math.min(maxPenumpang, prev + 1))
                  }
                  style={{
                    opacity: jumlahPenumpang >= maxPenumpang ? 0.4 : 1,
                    width: '34px',
                    height: '34px',
                    borderRadius: '10px',
                    border: 0,
                    background: '#fff',
                    fontSize: '18px',
                    color: '#0F172A',
                    cursor: jumlahPenumpang >= maxPenumpang ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,.1)',
                  }}
                >
                  +
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' && (
            <Input
              label="Tanggal & jam berangkat"
              type="datetime-local"
              value={jadwalAt}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setJadwalAt(e.target.value);
                if (error) setError('');
              }}
            />
          )}

          <div
            style={{
              color: '#5B6678',
              fontSize: '11.5px',
              marginTop: '-4px',
            }}
          >
            Pilih â€œJadwalâ€ untuk berangkat di waktu mendatang, lalu tentukan
            tanggal dan jamnya.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Harga Tawaran */}
        <Card>
          <label style={{ display: 'block' }}>
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#475569',
                marginBottom: '6px',
              }}
            >
              Harga yang kamu tawarkan
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                height: '52px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#F5F6F8',
                padding: '0 14px',
              }}
            >
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#5B6678' }}>Rp</span>
              <input
                aria-label="Tawaran harga"
                value={formatRibuan(ongkirDigits)}
                onChange={handleOngkirChange}
                inputMode="numeric"
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  border: 0,
                  outline: 0,
                  background: 'transparent',
                  font: "800 20px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  color: '#0F172A',
                  minWidth: 0,
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </label>
          <div
            style={{
              color: '#5B6678',
              fontSize: '11.5px',
              marginTop: '8px',
            }}
          >
            Driver bisa langsung menerima, atau menawar balik.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Bawah */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '12px 20px 20px',
          flex: 'none',
          background: '#F5F6F8',
        }}
      >
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? 'Mencari driver...' : 'Cari driver'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\customer\CustomerHapusAkun.jsx
==================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';

const ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'pending',
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

export default function CustomerHapusAkun() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [nimConfirm, setNimConfirm] = useState('');
  const [hasActiveOrders, setHasActiveOrders] = useState(false);
  const [checkingOrders, setCheckingOrders] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const checkActiveOrders = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const active = list.some((item) =>
          ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
        );
        if (isMounted) {
          setHasActiveOrders(active);
        }
      } catch {
        // Biarkan pengecekan server menangani saat endpoint Langkah 3 aktif
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) {
          setCheckingOrders(false);
        }
      }
    };

    checkActiveOrders();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const expectedIdentifier = user?.nim || user?.email || '';
  const isConfirmed =
    expectedIdentifier !== '' &&
    nimConfirm.trim().toLowerCase() === expectedIdentifier.trim().toLowerCase();

  const canDelete = !checkingOrders && !hasActiveOrders && isConfirmed;

  const handleDeleteAccount = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    if (hasActiveOrders) {
      setError('Selesaikan atau batalkan pesanan aktif terlebih dahulu.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isConfirmed) {
      setError('Konfirmasi NIM tidak sesuai dengan akunmu.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await api.delete('/auth/me', { data: { nim: nimConfirm.trim() } });
      logout();
      navigate('/login', { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menghapus akun.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleDeleteAccount}
      noValidate
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to="/customer/profil"
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '17px',
            letterSpacing: '-.01em',
          }}
        >
          Hapus akun
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '10px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Peringatan Permanen */}
        <Card
          variant="danger"
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '8px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
            <svg
              width="20"
              height="20"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="#B91C1C"
              strokeWidth="2.2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01" />
              <path d="M10.3 3.8L1.8 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h17a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.8a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0z" />
            </svg>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
              Tindakan ini permanen
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ fontSize: '12.5px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
            Menghapus akun akan menghapus profil, data mahasiswa, dan seluruh
            riwayat pesanan AnJem maupun JasTip secara permanen dan tidak
            dapat dipulihkan.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Peringatan jika masih ada pesanan aktif */}
        {hasActiveOrders && (
          <Card
            style={{
              background: '#FEF6E7',
              boxShadow: '0 0 0 1.5px #F59E0B',
              color: '#6B4308',
              fontSize: '12.5px',
              fontWeight: 600,
            }}
          >
            Kamu masih memiliki pesanan yang sedang berjalan. Selesaikan atau
            batalkan pesanan aktif sebelum menghapus akun.
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* Kartu Konfirmasi Identitas */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '10px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Konfirmasi penghapusan</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
            Ketik{' '}
            <strong style={{ color: '#0F172A' }}>{expectedIdentifier}</strong>{' '}
            di bawah ini untuk melanjutkan.
          </div>

          <Input
            variant="bordered"
            label={user?.nim ? 'Konfirmasi NIM' : 'Konfirmasi Email'}
            placeholder={expectedIdentifier}
            value={nimConfirm}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setNimConfirm(e.target.value);
              if (error) setError('');
            }}
          />
        </Card>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Bawah */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '12px 20px 20px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '10px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant="danger"
          disabled={!canDelete || submitting}
        >
          {submitting ? 'Menghapus akun...' : 'Hapus akun permanen'}
        </Button>

        <Button to="/customer/profil" variant="outline">
          Batal
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}




==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\customer\CustomerHistory.jsx
==================================================

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';

const HISTORY_STATUSES = ['completed', 'cancelled'];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  return (Number(value) || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatTanggal(isoString) {
  if (!isoString) return 'Hari ini';
  const date = new Date(isoString);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'Hari ini';
  return date.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

function readCache(key) {
  try {
    const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || '[]');
    return Array.isArray(cached) ? cached : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function saveCache(key, data) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Cache riwayat tidak dapat disimpan:', error);
  }
}

export default function CustomerHistory() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  const cacheKey = `anjemdips:history:customer:${userId || 'guest'}`;

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState(() => readCache(cacheKey));
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const fetchHistory = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!userId) {
      setOrders([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    // Tampilkan cache terlebih dahulu.
    const cached = readCache(cacheKey);
    setOrders(cached);
    setLoading(false);

    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];

      const historyList = list.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.customer_id === userId &&
          HISTORY_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
      );

      // Jangan menimpa cache jika API tidak mengembalikan riwayat.
      if (historyList.length > 0) {
        setOrders(historyList);
        saveCache(cacheKey, historyList);
      } else if (cached.length > 0) {
        setOrders(cached);
      } else {
        setOrders([]);
      }

      setError('');
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Gagal mengambil riwayat dari server:', err);
      setOrders(cached);
      setError('Tidak ada koneksi. Menampilkan riwayat terakhir yang tersimpan.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [userId, cacheKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchHistory();
  }, [fetchHistory]);

  const handleDeleteOrder = async (orderId) => {
    setError('');
    setDeletingId(orderId);

    try {
      await api.delete(`/orders/${orderId}`);

      setOrders((prev) => {
        const next = prev.filter((item) => item.id !== orderId);
        saveCache(cacheKey, next);
        return next;
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menghapus riwayat pesanan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setDeletingId(null);
    }
  };

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter((item) => {
    if (filter === 'completed') return item.status === 'completed';
    if (filter === 'cancelled') return item.status === 'cancelled';
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          History
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Riwayat perjalanan dan titipan.
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            background: '#E8EBEF',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            padding: '3px',
            gap: '3px',
            marginTop: '14px',
          }}
        >
          {[
            { key: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
            { key: 'completed', label: 'Selesai' },
            { key: 'cancelled', label: 'Dibatalkan' },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const active = filter === tab.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFilter(tab.key)}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '36px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  border: 0,
                  background: active ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: active ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                  font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  boxShadow: active ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)' : 'none',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '14px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat riwayat...
          </Card>
        ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              alignItems: 'center',
              textAlign: 'center',
              gap: '6px',
              padding: '24px 16px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
              Belum ada riwayat pesanan
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              Pesanan AnJem atau JasTip yang sudah selesai atau dibatalkan akan
              muncul di sini.
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          filteredOrders.map((order) => {
            const isJastip = order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
            const isMotor = order.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor';
            const vehicleLabel = isMotor ? 'Motor' : 'Mobil';
            const isCompleted = order.status === 'completed';

            const hargaBarang = Number(order.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;
            const ongkir = Number(order.ongkir) || 0;
            const totalHarga = isJastip ? hargaBarang + ongkir : ongkir;

            const repeatRoute = isJastip
              ? '/customer/jastip'
              : '/customer/anjem';

            return (
              <Card
                key={order.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '10px',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '10px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: '38px',
                      height: '38px',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      background: isJastip ? '#FDE9B8' : '#E6F4F2',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      color: isJastip ? '#6B4308' : '#0F766E',
                      flex: 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    {isJastip ? (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <path d="M5 8h14l-1 12H6z" />
                        <path d="M9 8a3 3 0 0 1 6 0" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : isMotor ? (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <circle cx="6" cy="16" r="3" />
                        <circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3" />
                        <path d="M6 16l4-7h5l3 7M9 9H7" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <path d="M4 16v-4l2-5h12l2 5v4zM4 16v3M20 16v3" />
                      </svg>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
                      {isJastip ? 'JasTip' : `AnJem Â· ${vehicleLabel}`}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11.5px' }}>
                      {formatTanggal(order.created_at)}
                      {order.driver?.nama ? ` Â· ${order.driver.nama}` : ''}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <Chip variant={isCompleted ? 'success' : 'danger'}>
                    {isCompleted ? 'Selesai' : 'Dibatalkan'}
                  </Chip>
                </div>

                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    gap: '6px',
                    paddingLeft: '2px',
                  }}

                      style={{
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: '12.5px',
                        color: '#0F172A',
                      }}
                    >
                      {order.nama_barang}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '8px',
                        height: '8px',
                        borderRadius: '99px',
                        background: '#0F766E',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
                      {order.titik_jemput}
                    </span>

                        background: '#F59E0B',
                        flex: 'none',

                      Rp {formatRibuan(totalHarga)}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                    {!isCompleted && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={deletingId === order.id}

                          padding: '0 12px',
                          borderRadius: '10px',
                          border: 0,
                          background: '#fff',
                          boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
                          color: '#B91C1C',
                          font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",

                            deletingId === order.id ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        }}
                      >
                        {deletingId === order.id ? 'Menghapus...' : 'Hapus'}
                      </button>
                    )}

                    <Link
                      to={repeatRoute}
                      style={{
                        height: '36px',
                        padding: '0 14px',

                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        textDecoration: 'none',
                      }}
                    >
                      Pesan lagi
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      <BottomNav role="customer" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\customer\CustomerJastip.jsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';

function formatRibuan(digits) {
  if (!digits) return '';
  return Number(digits).toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export default function CustomerJastip() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [namaBarang, setNamaBarang] = useState('');
  const [titikJemput, setTitikJemput] = useState('');
  const [titikAntar, setTitikAntar] = useState('');
  const [waktuBerangkat, setWaktuBerangkat] = useState('sekarang'); // 'sekarang' | 'jadwal'
  const [jadwalAt, setJadwalAt] = useState('');
  const [hargaBarangDigits, setHargaBarangDigits] = useState('30000');
  const [ongkirDigits, setOngkirDigits] = useState('8000');
  const [catatan, setCatatan] = useState('');

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const numericHargaBarang = Number(hargaBarangDigits) || 0;
  const numericOngkir = Number(ongkirDigits) || 0;
  const perkiraanTotal = numericHargaBarang + numericOngkir;

  const handleHargaBarangChange = (e) => {
    const raw = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
    setHargaBarangDigits(raw);
    if (error) setError('');
  };

  const handleOngkirChange = (e) => {
    const raw = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
    setOngkirDigits(raw);
    if (error) setError('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    const cleanBarang = namaBarang.trim();
    const cleanBeli = titikJemput.trim();
    const cleanAntar = titikAntar.trim();

    if (!cleanBarang || !cleanBeli || !cleanAntar) {
      setError('Barang yang dititip, lokasi beli/ambil, dan tujuan wajib diisi.');
      return;
    }

    if (!numericOngkir || numericOngkir < 2000) {
      setError('Minimal tawaran upah driver adalah Rp 2.000.');
      return;
    }

    let jadwalIso = null;
    if (waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal') {
      const picked = new Date(jadwalAt);
      if (
        !jadwalAt ||
        Number.isNaN(picked.getTime()) ||
        picked.getTime() < Date.now()
      ) {
        setError(
          'Pilih tanggal dan jam yang valid (tidak di masa lalu).'
        );
        return;
      }
      jadwalIso = picked.toISOString();
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        jenis_layanan: 'jastip',
        nama_barang: cleanBarang,
        titik_jemput: cleanBeli,
        titik_antar: cleanAntar,
        waktu_berangkat: waktuBerangkat,
        jadwal_at: jadwalIso,
        perkiraan_harga_barang: numericHargaBarang,
        ongkir: numericOngkir,
        catatan: catatan.trim(),
      };

      const res = await api.post('/orders', payload);
      navigate('/customer/mencari', {
        state: { order: res.data?.data },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal membuat pesanan JasTip. Coba lagi.'
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      noValidate
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to="/customer"
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '17px',
            letterSpacing: '-.01em',
          }}
        >
          Titip barang (JasTip)
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '6px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Detail Titipan & Lokasi */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <Input
            label="Barang yang dititip"
            placeholder="Contoh: 2 porsi nasi goreng, tanpa pedas"
            value={namaBarang}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setNamaBarang(e.target.value);
              if (error) setError('');
            }}
          />

          <Input
            label="Beli / ambil di"
            placeholder="Nama tempat atau alamat"
            value={titikJemput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setTitikJemput(e.target.value);
              if (error) setError('');
            }}
          />

          <Input
            label="Antar ke"
            placeholder="Antar ke mana? (contoh: Gedung Teknik Komputer)"
            value={titikAntar}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setTitikAntar(e.target.value);
              if (error) setError('');
            }}
          />

          <div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#475569',
                marginBottom: '6px',
              }}
            >
              Waktu
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                background: '#F1F3F6',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                padding: '3px',
                gap: '3px',
              }}
            >
              <div
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                aria-pressed={waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang'}
                onClick={() => setWaktuBerangkat('sekarang')}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    setWaktuBerangkat('sekarang');
                  }
                }}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '38px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: '12px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  background:
                    waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang' ? '#8A4B04' : '#5B6678',
                  boxShadow:
                    waktuBerangkat === 'sekarang'
                      ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                      : 'none',
                }}
              >
                Sekarang
              </div>

              <div
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                aria-pressed={waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal'}
                onClick={() => setWaktuBerangkat('jadwal')}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    setWaktuBerangkat('jadwal');
                  }
                }}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '38px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: '12px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  background:
                    waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' ? '#8A4B04' : '#5B6678',
                  boxShadow:
                    waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal'
                      ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)'
                      : 'none',
                }}
              >
                Jadwalkan
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {waktuBerangkat === 'jadwal' && (
            <Input
              label="Tanggal & jam"
              type="datetime-local"
              value={jadwalAt}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setJadwalAt(e.target.value);
                if (error) setError('');
              }}
            />
          )}
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Harga Barang & Upah Driver */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px' }}>
            <label style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: '11px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#475569',
                  marginBottom: '6px',
                }}
              >
                Perkiraan harga barang
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '8px',
                  height: '44px',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  background: '#F5F6F8',
                  padding: '0 12px',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontWeight: 700 }}>Rp</span>
                <input
                  aria-label="Perkiraan harga barang"
                  value={formatRibuan(hargaBarangDigits)}
                  onChange={handleHargaBarangChange}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    minWidth: 0,
                    border: 0,
                    outline: 0,
                    background: 'transparent',
                    font: "800 15px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                    color: '#0F172A',
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            </label>

            <label style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: '11px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: '#475569',
                  marginBottom: '6px',
                }}
              >
                Upah driver (tawaran)
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '8px',
                  height: '44px',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  background: '#F5F6F8',
                  padding: '0 12px',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontWeight: 700 }}>Rp</span>
                <input
                  aria-label="Upah driver (tawaran)"
                  value={formatRibuan(ongkirDigits)}
                  onChange={handleOngkirChange}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    minWidth: 0,
                    border: 0,
                    outline: 0,
                    background: 'transparent',
                    font: "800 15px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                    color: '#0F172A',
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            </label>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
              background: '#FEF6E7',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              padding: '10px 12px',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#6B4308', fontWeight: 600 }}>
              Perkiraan total
            </span>
            <span
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '15px',
                color: '#4A2C03',
              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(String(perkiraanTotal))}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11.5px' }}>
            Driver bisa menerima upahmu atau menawar balik sebelum membeli.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Catatan */}
        <Card style={{ display: 'block' }}>
          <Input
            label="Catatan untuk driver (opsional)"
            placeholder="Misal: tunggu di depan gerbang"
            value={catatan}
            onChange={(e) => setCatatan(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Card>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Bawah */}
      <div style={{ padding: '12px 20px 20px', flex: 'none' }}>
        <Button type="submit" variant="amber" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? 'Mencari driver...' : 'Cari driver'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\customer\CustomerLacak.jsx
==================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import { waktuLabel } from '../../utils/format';

const ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
  'pending',
];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatWaUrl(phone, text) {
  if (!phone) return null;
  let cleaned = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleaned.startsWith('0')) {
    cleaned = `62${cleaned.slice(1)}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${cleaned}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

export default function CustomerLacak() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const [secondsAgo, setSecondsAgo] = useState(3);
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Sinkronisasi status order & posisi driver secara berkala
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const syncOrder = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const target = order?.id
          ? list.find((item) => item.id === order.id)
          : list.find((item) => ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status));

        if (!isMounted || !target) return;
        setOrder(target);
        setSecondsAgo(1);
      } catch {
        // Pertahankan data terakhir saat gangguan jaringan sementara
      }
    };

    syncOrder();
    const pollInterval = setInterval(syncOrder, 5000);
    const tickInterval = setInterval(() => {
      setSecondsAgo((prev) => (prev >= 5 ? 1 : prev + 1));
    }, 1000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(pollInterval);
      clearInterval(tickInterval);
    };
  }, [order?.id]);

  const isOnWay = ['on_progress', 'on_the_way', 'in_progress'].includes(
    order?.status
  );
  const isCompleted = order?.status === 'completed';
  const isCancelled = order?.status === 'cancelled';
  const canCancel = ['pending', 'accepted'].includes(order?.status);

  const statusSubtitle = isCompleted
    ? 'Pesanan telah selesai'
    : isOnWay
    ? 'Driver sedang mengantar'
    : 'Driver sedang menjemput';

  const etaText = isCompleted
    ? 'Selesai'
    : isOnWay
    ? 'Dalam perjalanan'
    : 'Driver menuju lokasimu';

  const distanceText = isCompleted ? 'Tiba' : isOnWay ? 'Mengantar' : 'Menjemput';
  const activeStepCount = isCompleted ? 4 : isOnWay ? 3 : 2;

  const driverName = order?.driver?.nama || 'Driver';
  const driverInitial = (driverName.trim()[0] || 'D').toUpperCase();
  const isMotor =
    (order?.driver?.jenis_kendaraan || order?.jenis_kendaraan) === 'motor';
  const vehicleLabel = isMotor ? 'Motor' : 'Mobil';
  const platNomor = order?.driver?.plat_nomor || '-';

  const isJastip = order?.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
  const hargaBarang = Number(order?.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;
  const ongkir = Number(order?.ongkir) || 0;
  const agreedPrice = isJastip ? hargaBarang + ongkir : ongkir;

  const driverWa = formatWaUrl(
    order?.driver?.no_wa,
    `Halo kak ${driverName}, saya pemesan AnjemDips.`
  );

  const handleCancelOrder = async () => {
    if (!order?.id) {
      navigate('/customer');
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setCancelling(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/status`, {
        status: 'cancelled',
      });
      navigate('/customer');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
          'Pesanan tidak dapat dibatalkan pada tahap ini.'
      );
    } finally {
      setCancelling(false);
    }
  };

  // Layar khusus bila pesanan dibatalkan (mis. oleh driver)
  if (isCancelled) {
    return (
      <div
        className="pwa-shell"
        style={{
          minHeight: '844px',
          boxSizing: 'border-box',
          background: '#F5F6F8',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          gap: '12px',
          padding: '20px',
          textAlign: 'center',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '18px' }}>
          Pesanan dibatalkan
        </div>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>
          Pesanan ini telah dibatalkan. Kamu bisa membuat pesanan baru.
        </div>
        <Link
          to="/customer"
          style={{
            marginTop: '8px',
            height: '44px',
            padding: '0 24px',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            background: '#0F766E',
            color: '#fff',
            fontWeight: 700,
            display: 'inline-flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            textDecoration: 'none',
          }}
        >
          Kembali ke beranda
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#E9EEF0',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Peta Rute Driver */}
      <svg
        width="390"
        height="600"
        viewBox="0 0 390 600"
        fill="none"
        aria-hidden="true"
        style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0 }}
      >
        <path
          d="M-10 460 C90 420 150 330 230 350 S350 410 400 290"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="16"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path
          d="M130 -10 C160 110 110 220 170 350 S230 520 210 610"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="10"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path d="M-10 170 L400 130" stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="9" />
        <path
          d="M290 -10 C310 70 270 150 320 270"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="7"
        />
        <rect x="310" y="370" width="70" height="70" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <rect x="18" y="220" width="80" height="90" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <path
          d="M120 120 C130 200 160 280 190 345"
          stroke="#0F766E"
          strokeWidth="6"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <circle
          cx="190"
          cy="345"
          r="9"
          fill="#fff"
          stroke="#0F766E"
          strokeWidth="3"
        />
        <circle cx="190" cy="345" r="3.5" fill="#0F766E" />
        <g transform="translate(120 120)">
          <circle r="20" fill="#0F766E" fillOpacity=".18" />
          <circle
            r="14"
            fill="#0F766E"
            stroke="#fff"
            strokeWidth="3"
          />
          <path
            d="M-5 2v-3l1.5-3.5h7L8 -1v3zM-5 2v2M8 2v2"
            stroke="#fff"
            strokeWidth="1.4"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            transform="scale(.9)"
          />
        </g>
      </svg>

      {/* Tombol Kembali */}
      <Link
        to="/customer/pesanan"
        aria-label="Kembali"
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          left: '16px',
          top: '16px',
          width: '44px',
          height: '44px',
          borderRadius: '14px',
          background: '#fff',
          boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(15,23,42,.12)',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          color: '#0F172A',
        }}
      >
        <svg
          width="20"
          height="20"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
        </svg>
      </Link>

      {/* Pil Status Pembaruan */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          right: '16px',

                <path d="M5 4h4l2 5-2.5 1.5a11 11 0 0 0 5 5L15 13l5 2v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2A16 16 0 0 1 3 6a2 2 0 0 1 2-2z" />
              </svg>
              Hubungi
            </span>
          )}

          {canCancel && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={cancelling}
              onClick={handleCancelOrder}
              style={{
                height: '46px',
                borderRadius: '14px',
                border: 0,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                font: "700 13px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
                flex: 1,
                background: '#fff',
                color: '#B91C1C',
                boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
                cursor: cancelling ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                opacity: cancelling ? 0.6 : 1,
              }}
            >
              {cancelling ? 'Membatalkan...' : 'Batalkan'}
            </button>
          )}

          {isCompleted && (
            <Link
              to="/customer/history"
              style={{
                height: '46px',
                borderRadius: '14px',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                fontWeight: 700,
                fontSize: '13px',
                textDecoration: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
                flex: 1,
                background: '#E6F4F2',
                color: '#0B5F59',
              }}
            >
              Lihat riwayat
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import { waktuLabel } from '../../utils/format';

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export default function CustomerMencari() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [offerCount, setOfferCount] = useState(0);

  // Ambil order pending terbaru jika halaman dibuka langsung / di-refresh,
  // sekaligus pantau berkala jika driver sudah menerima pesanan
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const syncOrder = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];

        const target = order?.id
          ? list.find((item) => item.id === order.id)
          : list.find((item) => item.status === 'pending');

        if (!isMounted || !target) return;

        setOrder(target);

        if (target.status === 'pending') {
          try {
            const offersRes = await api.get(`/orders/${target.id}/offers`);
            const offerList = Array.isArray(offersRes.data?.data)
              ? offersRes.data.data
              : [];
            if (isMounted) {
              setOfferCount(
                offerList.filter(
                  (o) => o.offered_by === 'driver' && o.status === 'pending'
                ).length
              );
            }
          } catch {
            // Abaikan error jaringan sementara saat memuat tawaran
          }
        }

        if (
          ['accepted', 'on_progress', 'on_the_way', 'in_progress'].includes(
            target.status
          )
        ) {
          navigate('/customer/lacak', {
            replace: true,
            state: { order: target },
          });
        }
      } catch {
        // Abaikan error jaringan sementara saat polling
      }
    };

    syncOrder();
    const interval = setInterval(syncOrder, 5000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [order?.id, navigate]);

  const isJastip = order?.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
  const backPath = isJastip ? '/customer/jastip' : '/customer/anjem';

  const vehicleLabel =
    order?.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor' ? 'Motor' : 'Mobil';
  const penumpangCount = order?.jumlah_penumpang || 1;
  const waktuText = waktuLabel(order);

  const handleCancel = async () => {
    if (!order?.id) {
      navigate(backPath);
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setCancelling(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/status`, {
        status: 'cancelled',
      });
      navigate(backPath);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal membatalkan pesanan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setCancelling(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#E9EEF0',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Peta Radar Latar */}
      <svg
        width="390"
        height="560"
        viewBox="0 0 390 560"
        fill="none"
        aria-hidden="true"
        style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0 }}
      >
        <path
          d="M-10 420 C90 380 140 300 220 320 S350 380 400 260"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="16"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path
          d="M120 -10 C150 100 100 200 160 330 S220 480 200 570"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="10"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path d="M-10 150 L400 110" stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="9" />
        <path
          d="M270 -10 C290 60 250 140 300 250"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="7"
        />
        <rect x="300" y="330" width="70" height="60" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <rect x="20" y="200" width="70" height="80" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <circle cx="195" cy="300" r="130" fill="#0F766E" fillOpacity=".07" />
        <circle cx="195" cy="300" r="88" fill="#0F766E" fillOpacity=".1" />
        <circle cx="195" cy="300" r="48" fill="#0F766E" fillOpacity=".16" />
        <circle
          cx="195"
          cy="300"
          r="12"
          fill="#0F766E"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="4"
        />
        <circle
          cx="260"
          cy="240"
          r="7"
          fill="#fff"
          stroke="#5B6678"
          strokeWidth="2"
        />
        <circle
          cx="130"
          cy="350"
          r="7"
          fill="#fff"
          stroke="#5B6678"
          strokeWidth="2"
        />
        <circle
          cx="230"
          cy="380"
          r="7"
          fill="#fff"
          stroke="#5B6678"
          strokeWidth="2"
        />
      </svg>

      {/* Tombol Kembali Mengambang */}
      <Link
        to={backPath}
        aria-label="Kembali"
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          left: '16px',

              }}
            />
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>
              {order?.titik_jemput || 'Titik jemput'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span
              style={{
                width: '10px',
                height: '10px',
                borderRadius: '3px',
                background: '#F59E0B',
                flex: 'none',
              }}
            />
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>
              {order?.titik_antar || 'Tujuan'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#E2E6EB' }} />

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>
              {isJastip
                ? `JasTip Â· ${order?.nama_barang || 'Titip barang'} Â· ${waktuText}`
                : `${vehicleLabel} Â· ${penumpangCount} penumpang Â· ${waktuText}`}
            </span>
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
              Rp {formatRibuan(order?.ongkir ?? 15000)}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            color: '#5B6678',
            fontSize: '12px',
            marginTop: '12px',
            textAlign: 'center',
          }}
        >
          Biasanya 1â€“2 menit. Kamu akan melihat tawaran begitu driver menjawab.
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              marginTop: '10px',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
              textAlign: 'center',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', marginTop: '14px' }}>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="dangerOutline"
            disabled={cancelling}
            onClick={handleCancel}
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
          >
            {cancelling ? 'Membatalkan...' : 'Batalkan'}
          </Button>
          <Button
            to="/customer/tawaran"
            state={order ? { order } : undefined}
            variant="primary"
            disabled={offerCount === 0}
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
          >
            {offerCount > 0
              ? `Lihat tawaran (${offerCount})`
              : 'Belum ada tawaran'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
