import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';
import { waktuLabel } from '../../utils/format';

const ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'pending',
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatWaUrl(phone, text) {
  if (!phone) return null;
  let cleaned = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleaned.startsWith('0')) {
    cleaned = `62${cleaned.slice(1)}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${cleaned}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

export default function CustomerPesanan() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [cancellingId, setCancellingId] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const fetchActiveOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
      const activeList = list.filter((item) =>
        ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
      );
      setOrders(activeList);
      setError('');
    } catch {
      setError('Koneksi terputus. Menampilkan data pesanan terakhir yang berhasil dimuat.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchActiveOrders();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchActiveOrders, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [fetchActiveOrders]);

  const handleCancelOrder = async (orderId) => {
    setError('');
    setCancellingId(orderId);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${orderId}/status`, {
        status: 'cancelled',
      });
      await fetchActiveOrders();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal membatalkan pesanan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setCancellingId(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          Pesanan
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Pesanan yang sedang berjalan.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Daftar Pesanan Aktif */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '16px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat pesanan...
          </Card>
        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              alignItems: 'center',
              textAlign: 'center',
              gap: '8px',
              padding: '22px 16px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
              Belum ada pesanan aktif
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              Pesanan AnJem atau JasTip yang sedang berjalan akan tampil di
              sini.
            </div>
            <Link
              to="/customer/anjem"
              style={{
                marginTop: '6px',
                height: '40px',
                padding: '0 18px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#0F766E',
                color: '#fff',
                fontWeight: 700,
                fontSize: '13px',
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                textDecoration: 'none',
              }}
            >
              Buat pesanan
            </Link>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          orders.map((order) => {
            const isJastip = order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
            const isMotor = order.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor';
            const vehicleLabel = isMotor ? 'Motor' : 'Mobil';
            const isPending = order.status === 'pending';
            const isOnWay = [

            const hargaBarang = Number(order.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;

                      }}
                    >

                        <svg
                          width="20"

                          fill="none"

                      ) : isMotor ? (

                      )}
                    </div>

                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
                        {isJastip ? 'JasTip' : `AnJem Â· ${vehicleLabel}`}

                      <div

                    }
                  >

                  </span>
                  <span

                        : undefined
                    }
                  >
                    Di jalan

                  <span>Selesai</span>

                >

                    {driverPlat ? ` Â· ${driverPlat}` : ''}
                  </span>

                    Rp {formatRibuan(totalHarga)}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Tombol Aksi */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>
                  <Link
                    to="/customer/lacak"

                    style={{

                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      display: 'flex',

                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      fontSize: '13px',
                      textDecoration: 'none',
                      boxSizing: 'border-box',
                      flex: 1,
                      background: '#0F766E',
                      color: '#fff',
                    }}
                  >
                    Lacak driver
                  </Link>

                  {driverWa ? (
                    <a
                      href={driverWa}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      style={{
                        height: '44px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        textDecoration: 'none',
                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                        flex: 1,
                        background: '#fff',

                        boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                      }}
                    >
                      Hubungi
                    </a>
                  ) : (
                    <span
                      aria-disabled="true"
                      style={{
                        opacity: 0.5,
                        height: '44px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        textDecoration: 'none',
                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                        flex: 1,
                        background: '#fff',
                        color: '#0F172A',
                        boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                      }}
                    >
                      Hubungi
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Navigation */}
      <BottomNav role="customer" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\customer\CustomerProfil.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';

export default function CustomerProfil() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ completed: 0, total: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const loadStats = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const completed = list.filter(
          (item) => item.status === 'completed'
        ).length;
        if (isMounted) {
          setStats({ completed, total: list.length });
        }
      } catch {
        // Abaikan error statistik jika offline sementara
      }
    };

    loadStats();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const displayName = user?.nama || 'Mahasiswa';
  const initial = (displayName.trim()[0] || 'M').toUpperCase();

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate('/login', { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          Profil
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Informasi akun dan pengaturan.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '16px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Identitas Utama */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '14px',
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              width: '54px',
              height: '54px',
              borderRadius: '99px',
              background: '#0F766E',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontWeight: 800,
              fontSize: '20px',
              flex: 'none',
            }}
          >
            {initial}
          </div>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '16px',
                letterSpacing: '-.01em',
              }}
            >
              {displayName}
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
              {user?.nim ? `NIM ${user.nim}` : user?.email || '-'}
            </div>
          </div>
          <Chip variant="teal">Pemesan</Chip>
        </Card>

        {/* Ringkasan Aktivitas */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 14px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Pesanan selesai
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '20px',
                color: '#0F766E',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              {stats.completed}
            </div>
          </Card>

          <Card style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 14px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Total transaksi
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '20px',
                color: '#0F172A',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              {stats.total}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* Kartu Detail Akun */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '14px' }}>Data akun</div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>NIM</span>
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{user?.nim || '-'}</span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />

          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>Email</span>
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{user?.email || '-'}</span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />

          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>Nomor WhatsApp</span>
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{user?.no_wa || '-'}</span>
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Pengaturan & Zona Berbahaya */}
        <Card style={{ padding: '4px 14px' }}>
          <Link
            to="/customer/hapus-akun"
            style={{
              height: '46px',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              textDecoration: 'none',
              color: '#B91C1C',
              fontWeight: 700,
            }}
          >
            <span>Hapus akun permanen</span>
            <svg
              width="18"
              height="18"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
            </svg>
          </Link>
        </Card>

        {/* Tombol Keluar */}
        <div style={{ marginTop: '4px' }}>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="dangerOutline"
            onClick={handleLogout}
          >
            Keluar dari akun
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Navigation */}
      <BottomNav role="customer" />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import Button from '../../components/Button';

const EXTRA_PRICE_OPTIONS = [3000, 5000, 8000];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function OfferCard({
  order,
  offer,
  busy,
  onAccept,
  onCounter,
  onReject,
  onError,
}) {
  const driver = offer.driver || {};
  const driverName = driver.nama || 'Driver';
  const driverInitial = (driverName.trim()[0] || 'D').toUpperCase();
  const isMotorDriver = driver.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor';
  const vehicleLabel = isMotorDriver ? 'Motor' : 'Mobil';
  const capacity = isMotorDriver ? 1 : Number(driver.kapasitas_penumpang) || 4;

  const originalPassengers = Number(order?.jumlah_penumpang) || 1;
  const offerPassengers = Number(offer.adjusted_passengers) || originalPassengers;
  const isAnjem = order?.jenis_layanan === 'anjem';
  const isOverCapacity = isAnjem && offerPassengers > capacity;
  const excessCount = Math.max(0, offerPassengers - capacity);

  const isDriverOffer = offer.offered_by === 'driver';
  const baseCustomerOffer = Number(order?.ongkir) || 0;

  const [showCounter, setShowCounter] = useState(false);
  const [counterDigits, setCounterDigits] = useState(String(offer.offered_price));
  const [reducedPassengers, setReducedPassengers] = useState(
    Math.min(offerPassengers, capacity)
  );
  const [selectedExtra, setSelectedExtra] = useState(5000);

  // Tawaran pemesan sendiri yang masih menunggu jawaban driver
  if (!isDriverOffer) {
    return (
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div
          style={{
            width: '46px',
            height: '46px',
            borderRadius: '99px',
            background: '#0F766E',
            color: '#fff',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            fontWeight: 800,
            flex: 'none',
          }}
        >
          {driverInitial}
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>{driverName}</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
            {offer.status === 'rejected'
              ? `Tawaranmu Rp ${formatRibuan(offer.offered_price)} ditolak driver. Tunggu tawaran baru dari driver.`
              : `Menunggu jawaban atas tawaranmu Rp ${formatRibuan(offer.offered_price)}`}
          </div>
        </div>
        <Chip variant={offer.status === 'rejected' ? 'danger' : 'warning'}>
          {offer.status === 'rejected' ? 'Ditolak' : 'Menunggu'}
        </Chip>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  const handleSendCounter = () => {
    const parsed = Number(counterDigits);
    if (!parsed || parsed < 2000) {
      onError('Minimal tawaran harga adalah Rp 2.000.');
      return;
    }
    onCounter(offer.driver_id, parsed, null);
    setShowCounter(false);
  };

  const adjustedNewOffer = baseCustomerOffer + selectedExtra;

  return (
    <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
      {/* Profil driver */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div
          style={{
            width: '46px',
            height: '46px',
            borderRadius: '99px',
            background: '#0F766E',
            color: '#fff',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '16px',
            flex: 'none',
          }}
        >
          {driverInitial}
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>{driverName}</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
            {vehicleLabel} Â· {driver.plat_nomor || '-'}
            {!isMotorDriver ? ` Â· Maks ${capacity} org` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
        <Chip variant={driver.is_online ? 'teal' : 'off'}>
          {driver.is_online ? 'Online' : 'Offline'}
        </Chip>
      </div>

      {offer.note && (
        <div
          style={{
            background: '#F5F6F8',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            padding: '8px 12px',
            fontSize: '12px',
            color: '#475569',
          }}
        >
          <strong>Catatan driver:</strong> {offer.note}
        </div>
      )}

      {isOverCapacity ? (
        <>
          <div
            style={{
              background: '#FFF6F6',
              boxShadow: '0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
              borderRadius: '14px',
              padding: '12px',
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              gap: '10px',
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
                Kapasitas kendaraan
              </div>
              <Chip variant="danger">Kelebihan {excessCount} orang</Chip>
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
              Penumpangmu ({offerPassengers}) melebihi kapasitas ({capacity}). Kurangi
              penumpang atau tawarkan harga ekstra.
            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                height: '44px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#fff',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                padding: '0 5px',
                boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
              }}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Kurangi"
                onClick={() => setReducedPassengers((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
                style={{
                  width: '34px',
                  height: '34px',
                  borderRadius: '10px',
                  border: 0,
                  background: '#F5F6F8',
                  fontSize: '18px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                âˆ’
              </button>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
                {offerPassengers} â†’ {reducedPassengers}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Tambah"
                onClick={() =>
                  setReducedPassengers((p) => Math.min(offerPassengers, p + 1))
                }
                style={{
                  width: '34px',
                  height: '34px',
                  borderRadius: '10px',
                  border: 0,
                  background: '#F5F6F8',
                  fontSize: '18px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                +
              </button>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
              {EXTRA_PRICE_OPTIONS.map((extra) => {
                const isSelected = selectedExtra === extra;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={extra}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedExtra(extra)}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '40px',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      border: 0,
                      background: isSelected ? '#0F766E' : '#fff',
                      boxShadow: isSelected ? 'none' : 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                      font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      color: isSelected ? '#fff' : '#0F172A',
                      cursor: 'pointer',
                    }}
                  >
                    + Rp {formatRibuan(extra)}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
              }}
            >
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Total tawaran baru</span>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '18px', color: '#0F766E' }}>
                Rp {formatRibuan(adjustedNewOffer)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="primary"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() =>
              onCounter(offer.driver_id, adjustedNewOffer, reducedPassengers)
            }
          >
            {busy ? 'Mengirim...' : 'Kirim tawaran baru'}
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="dangerOutline"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => onReject(offer)}
          >
            Tolak tawaran
          </Button>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>Tawaranmu</span>
            <span
              style={{
                fontWeight: 700,
                textDecoration: 'line-through',
                color: '#5B6678',
              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(baseCustomerOffer)}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Tawaran driver</span>
            <span
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '22px',
                color: '#0F766E',
                letterSpacing: '-.02em',
              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(offer.offered_price)}
            </span>
          </div>

          {showCounter && (
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                height: '44px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#F5F6F8',
                padding: '0 12px',
              }}
            >
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#5B6678' }}>Rp</span>
              <input
                aria-label="Nominal tawar balik"
                inputMode="numeric"
                value={formatRibuan(counterDigits)}
                onChange={(e) => setCounterDigits(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''))}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  border: 0,
                  outline: 0,
                  background: 'transparent',
                  font: "800 16px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  color: '#0F172A',
                  minWidth: 0,
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="primary"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => onAccept(offer)}
          >
            {busy ? 'Memproses...' : `Setuju Rp ${formatRibuan(offer.offered_price)}`}
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => (showCounter ? handleSendCounter() : setShowCounter(true))}
          >
            {showCounter ? 'Kirim tawaran' : 'Tawar balik'}
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="dangerOutline"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => onReject(offer)}
          >
            Tolak tawaran
          </Button>
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function CustomerTawaran() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const [offers, setOffers] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busyDriverId, setBusyDriverId] = useState(null);
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const orderId = order?.id;

  const sync = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
      const target = orderId
        ? list.find((item) => item.id === orderId)
        : list.find((item) => item.status === 'pending');

      if (!target) return;
      setOrder(target);

      if (['accepted', 'on_progress'].includes(target.status)) {
        navigate('/customer/lacak', { replace: true, state: { order: target } });
        return;
      }

      if (target.status !== 'pending') {
        navigate('/customer', { replace: true });
        return;
      }

      const offersRes = await api.get(`/orders/${target.id}/offers`);
      const all = Array.isArray(offersRes.data?.data) ? offersRes.data.data : [];

      // Ambil tawaran terbaru per driver yang masih pending
      const seen = new Set();
      const latestPending = [];
      for (const offer of all) {
        if (seen.has(offer.driver_id)) continue;
        seen.add(offer.driver_id);
        if (
          offer.status === 'pending' ||
          (offer.status === 'rejected' && offer.offered_by === 'customer')
        ) {
          latestPending.push(offer);
        }
      }
      setOffers(latestPending);
    } catch {
      // Pertahankan data terakhir saat gangguan jaringan sementara
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [orderId, navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    sync();
    const interval = setInterval(sync, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [sync]);

  const handleAccept = async (offer) => {
    if (!order?.id) return;
    setError('');
    setBusyDriverId(offer.driver_id);
    try {
      const res = await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/offers/${offer.id}/accept`);
      navigate('/customer/lacak', {
        replace: true,
        state: { order: res.data?.data },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menyetujui tawaran.');
      await sync();
    } finally {
      setBusyDriverId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleCounter = async (driverId, price, passengers) => {
    if (!order?.id) return;
    setError('');
    setBusyDriverId(driverId);
    try {
      const payload = { driver_id: driverId, offered_price: price };
      if (passengers) payload.adjusted_passengers = passengers;
      await api.post(`/orders/${order.id}/offers`, payload);
      await sync();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal mengirim tawaran balik.');
    } finally {
      setBusyDriverId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleReject = async (offer) => {
    if (!order?.id) return;
    setError('');
    setBusyDriverId(offer.driver_id);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/offers/${offer.id}/reject`);
      await sync();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menolak tawaran.');
    } finally {
      setBusyDriverId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleCancelOrder = async () => {
    if (!order?.id) {
      navigate('/customer');
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setCancelling(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/status`, { status: 'cancelled' });
      navigate('/customer');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal membatalkan pesanan.');
    } finally {
      setCancelling(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to="/customer/mencari"
          state={order ? { order } : undefined}
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '17px', letterSpacing: '-.01em' }}>
          Tawaran driver
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '6px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
        }}
      >
        {/* Ringkasan order */}
        {order && (
          <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '10px',
                  height: '10px',
                  borderRadius: '99px',
                  background: '#0F766E',
                  flex: 'none',
                }}
              />
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{order.titik_jemput}</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '10px',
                  height: '10px',
                  borderRadius: '3px',
                  background: '#F59E0B',
                  flex: 'none',
                }}
              />
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{order.titik_antar}</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                color: '#5B6678',
              }}
            >
              <span>
                {order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip'
                  ? 'JasTip'
                  : `${order.jumlah_penumpang || 1} penumpang`}
              </span>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0F172A' }}>
                Tawaranmu Rp {formatRibuan(order.ongkir)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </Card>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat tawaran...
          </Card>
        ) : offers.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              textAlign: 'center',
              padding: '24px 16px',
              color: '#5B6678',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0F172A' }}>Belum ada tawaran</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Tawaran dari driver akan muncul di sini secara otomatis.
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          offers.map((offer) => (
            <OfferCard
              key={offer.id}
              order={order}
              offer={offer}
              busy={busyDriverId === offer.driver_id}
              onAccept={handleAccept}
              onCounter={handleCounter}
              onReject={handleReject}
              onError={setError}
            />
          ))
        )}

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol bawah */}
      <div style={{ padding: '12px 20px 20px', flex: 'none' }}>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="dangerOutline"
          disabled={cancelling}
          onClick={handleCancelOrder}
        >
          {cancelling ? 'Membatalkan...' : 'Batalkan pesanan'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\driver\DriverBeranda.jsx
==================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';
import { getDriverVehicleProfile } from '../../mocks/driverMocks';
import { formatRibuan, waktuLabel } from '../../utils/format';

const ACTIVE_TRIP_STATUSES = [
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

function isSameDay(isoDate) {
  if (!isoDate) return false;
  const target = new Date(isoDate);
  const now = new Date();
  return (
    target.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() &&
    target.getMonth() === now.getMonth() &&
    target.getDate() === now.getDate()
  );
}

export default function DriverBeranda() {
  const { user, updateUser } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(Boolean(user?.is_online));
  const [togglingStatus, setTogglingStatus] = useState(false);

  const [pendingOrders, setPendingOrders] = useState([]);
  const [offerMap, setOfferMap] = useState({}); // orderId -> tawaran terbaru driver ini
  const [activeTrip, setActiveTrip] = useState(null);
  const [todayStats, setTodayStats] = useState({ count: 0, earnings: 0 });
  const [loadingOrders, setLoadingOrders] = useState(true);
  const [acceptingId, setAcceptingId] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const vehicle = getDriverVehicleProfile(user);
  const vehicleLabel =
    vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil'
      ? 'Mobil'
      : vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor'
      ? 'Motor'
      : 'Kendaraan';
  const platLabel = vehicle.plat_nomor || 'plat belum diisi';

  const fetchOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];

      const incoming = list.filter((item) => item.status === 'pending');
      const running = list.find(
        (item) =>
          item.driver_id === user?.id &&
          ACTIVE_TRIP_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
      );

      const completedToday = list.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.driver_id === user?.id &&
          item.status === 'completed' &&
          isSameDay(item.updated_at || item.created_at)
      );

      const totalEarnings = completedToday.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.ongkir) || 0),
        0
      );

      // Ambil tawaran terbaru driver ini untuk tiap request (tawar balik / tawaran terkirim)
      const offerEntries = await Promise.all(
        incoming.map(async (item) => {
          try {
            const offersRes = await api.get(`/orders/${item.id}/offers`);
            const offerList = Array.isArray(offersRes.data?.data)
              ? offersRes.data.data
              : [];
            return [item.id, offerList[0] || null];
          } catch {
            return [item.id, null];
          }
        })
      );

      setPendingOrders(incoming);
      setOfferMap(Object.fromEntries(offerEntries));
      setActiveTrip(running || null);
      setTodayStats({
        count: completedToday.length,
        earnings: totalEarnings,
      });
    } catch {
      // Pertahankan data terakhir jika jaringan bermasalah sementara
    } finally {
      setLoadingOrders(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchOrders();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchOrders, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [fetchOrders]);

  const handleToggleOnline = async () => {
    if (togglingStatus) return;
    setTogglingStatus(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const next = !isOnline;
      const res = await api.patch('/driver/status', { is_online: next });
      updateUser(res.data.user);
      setIsOnline(next);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal mengubah status online.'
      );
    } finally {
      setTogglingStatus(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickAccept = async (order) => {
    setError('');
    setAcceptingId(order.id);
    try {
      const res = await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/accept`);
      navigate('/driver/pesanan', {
        state: { order: res.data?.data || order },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal mengambil pesanan ini.'
      );
      await fetchOrders();
    } finally {
      setAcceptingId(null);
    }
  };

  const displayName = user?.nama || 'Driver';
  const initial = (displayName.trim()[0] || 'D').toUpperCase();

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header Top */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '20px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: '42px',
              height: '42px',
              borderRadius: '99px',
              background: '#0F766E',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontWeight: 800,
              fontSize: '15px',
              flex: 'none',
            }}
          >
            {initial}
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>Halo,</div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '16px',
                letterSpacing: '-.01em',
              }}
            >
              {displayName}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Toggle Status OFF / ON */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleToggleOnline}
          disabled={togglingStatus}
          aria-pressed={isOnline}
          style={{
            height: '38px',
            padding: '0 14px',
            borderRadius: '99px',
            border: 0,
            background: isOnline ? '#DDF3E4' : '#E8EAEE',
            color: isOnline ? '#14532D' : '#475569',
            font: "800 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '8px',
            cursor: 'pointer',
          }}
        >
          <span
            style={{
              width: '9px',
              height: '9px',
              borderRadius: '99px',
              background: isOnline ? '#16A34A' : '#64748B',
            }}
          />
          {isOnline ? 'ON Â· Aktif' : 'OFF Â· Nonaktif'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '16px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {/* Banner: data kendaraan belum lengkap */}
        {!vehicle.lengkap && (
          <Card
            to="/driver/profil"
            style={{
              background: '#FEF6E7',
              boxShadow: '0 0 0 1.5px #F59E0B',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              gap: '12px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#4A2C03' }}>
                Lengkapi data kendaraan
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#6B4308' }}>
                Jenis kendaraan dan plat nomor wajib diisi sebelum bisa
                menerima atau menawar order.
              </div>
            </div>
            <span
              style={{
                background: '#F59E0B',
                color: '#3B2402',
                borderRadius: '99px',
                padding: '5px 11px',
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 800,
                flex: 'none',
              }}
            >
              Isi sekarang
            </span>
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* Kartu Kendaraan & Status Operasional */}
        {isOnline ? (
          <Card
            variant="soft"
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              gap: '12px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '38px',
                  height: '38px',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  background: '#0F766E',
                  color: '#fff',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  flex: 'none',
                }}
              >
                {vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil' ? (
                  <svg
                    width="19"
                    height="19"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    strokeWidth="1.9"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    <path d="M4 16v-4l2-5h12l2 5v4zM4 16v3M20 16v3" />
                  </svg>
                ) : (
                  <svg
                    width="19"
                    height="19"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    strokeWidth="1.9"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    <circle cx="6" cy="16" r="3" />
                    <circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3" />
                    <path d="M6 16l4-7h5l3 7M9 9H7" />
                  </svg>
                )}
              </div>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0B4F4A' }}>
                  {vehicle.lengkap ? 'Siap menerima order' : 'Status ON'}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0B5F59' }}>
                  {vehicleLabel} Â· {platLabel}
                  {vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil'
                    ? ` Â· Maks ${vehicle.kapasitas_penumpang} org`
                    : ''}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Chip variant="success">Online</Chip>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <Card
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              gap: '12px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '40px',
                  height: '40px',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  background: '#F1F3F6',

                  display: 'flex',

                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  flex: 'none',
                }}
              >
                <svg
                  width="20"
                  height="20"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="2"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <path d="M18.36 6.64a9 9 0 1 1-12.73 0" />
                  <line x1="12" y1="2" x2="12" y2="12" />
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '14px' }}>
                  Status kamu sedang OFF
                </div>
                <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                  Aktifkan status untuk mulai melihat dan mengambil request
                  mahasiswa.

              </div>
            </div>
            <Button

        {/* Ringkasan Pendapatan & Trip Hari Ini */}

              Pendapatan hari ini
            </div>

              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,

              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(todayStats.earnings)}
            </div>
          </Card>

          <Card style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 14px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Trip selesai
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '18px',
                color: '#0F172A',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              {todayStats.count} pesanan
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* Banner Pesanan yang Sedang Berjalan */}
        {activeTrip && (
          <Card
            to="/driver/pesanan"
            style={{
              background: '#0F766E',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              gap: '12px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#C9ECE8', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                Pesanan sedang berjalan
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  fontSize: '14px',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                }}
              >
                {activeTrip.customer?.nama || 'Pemesan'} Â·{' '}
                {activeTrip.titik_antar}
              </div>
            </div>
            <span
              style={{
                background: '#fff',
                color: '#0F766E',
                borderRadius: '99px',
                padding: '5px 11px',
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 800,
                flex: 'none',
              }}
            >
              Lanjutkan
            </span>
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* Section Header Request Masuk */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            marginTop: '2px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '14px' }}>
            Request masuk ({isOnline ? pendingOrders.length : 0})
          </div>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '11.5px', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            {vehicleLabel} Â· {platLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Daftar Request Masuk */}
        {!isOnline ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              textAlign: 'center',
              padding: '22px 16px',
              color: '#5B6678',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#0F172A' }}>
              Radar request nonaktif
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Ubah status menjadi ON di kanan atas untuk melihat permintaan
              masuk.
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : loadingOrders ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Mencari request di sekitarmu...
          </Card>
        ) : pendingOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              textAlign: 'center',
              padding: '24px 16px',
              color: '#5B6678',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0F172A' }}>
              Belum ada request baru
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Tetap aktifkan halaman ini, pesanan baru akan muncul otomatis.
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          pendingOrders.map((order) => {
            const isJastip = order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
            const orderVehicle =
              order.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil' ? 'Mobil' : 'Motor';

            const requestedPax = Number(order.jumlah_penumpang) || 1;
            const isMismatch =
              !isJastip &&
              Boolean(order.jenis_kendaraan) &&
              Boolean(vehicle.jenis_kendaraan) &&
              order.jenis_kendaraan !== vehicle.jenis_kendaraan;
            const isOverCapacity =
              !isJastip &&
              Boolean(vehicle.kapasitas_penumpang) &&
              requestedPax > vehicle.kapasitas_penumpang;

            const latestOffer = offerMap[order.id] || null;
            const hasCounter =
              latestOffer?.offered_by === 'customer' &&
              latestOffer?.status === 'pending';
            const hasMyBid =
              latestOffer?.offered_by === 'driver' &&
              latestOffer?.status === 'pending';

            const quickBlocked =
              !vehicle.lengkap || !isOnline || isMismatch || isOverCapacity;

            return (
              <Card
                key={order.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '10px',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    gap: '8px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: '8px',
                      minWidth: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    <Chip variant={isJastip ? 'warning' : 'teal'}>
                      {isJastip ? 'JasTip' : `AnJem Â· ${orderVehicle}`}
                    </Chip>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        fontSize: '11.5px',
                        color: '#5B6678',
                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                        textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                      }}
                    >
                      {waktuLabel(order)}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <span
                    style={{
                      fontWeight: 800,
                      fontSize: '15px',
                      color: '#0F766E',
                      flex: 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    Rp {formatRibuan(order.ongkir)}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Penanda status request */}
                {(isMismatch || isOverCapacity || hasCounter || hasMyBid) && (
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '6px' }}>
                    {isMismatch && <Chip variant="danger">Beda kendaraan</Chip>}
                    {isOverCapacity && (
                      <Chip variant="danger">Melebihi kapasitas</Chip>
                    )}
                    {hasCounter && (
                      <Chip variant="warning">
                        Tawar balik Rp {formatRibuan(latestOffer.offered_price)}
                      </Chip>
                    )}
                    {hasMyBid && (
                      <Chip variant="neutral">Tawaranmu terkirim</Chip>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Info Pemesan / Barang */}
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '13px' }}>
                  {order.customer?.nama || 'Mahasiswa'}
                  {isJastip && order.nama_barang
                    ? ` Â· ${order.nama_barang}`
                    : !isJastip && order.jumlah_penumpang
                    ? ` Â· ${order.jumlah_penumpang} penumpang`
                    : ''}
                </div>

                {/* Rute Jemput & Antar */}
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    gap: '6px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: '8px',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '8px',
                        height: '8px',
                        borderRadius: '99px',
                        background: '#0F766E',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
                      {order.titik_jemput}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: '8px',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '8px',
                        height: '8px',
                        borderRadius: '2px',
                        background: '#F59E0B',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
                      {order.titik_antar}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Tombol Detail & Terima */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                  <Link
                    to={`/driver/request/${order.id}`}
                    state={{ order }}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '40px',
                      borderRadius: '11px',
                      background: '#fff',
                      boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                      color: '#0F172A',
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      fontSize: '12.5px',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      textDecoration: 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    {hasCounter ? 'Lihat tawar balik' : 'Detail / Nego'}
                  </Link>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acceptingId === order.id || quickBlocked}
                    onClick={() => handleQuickAccept(order)}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '40px',
                      borderRadius: '11px',
                      border: 0,
                      background: quickBlocked ? '#E8EAEE' : '#0F766E',
                      color: quickBlocked ? '#64748B' : '#fff',
                      font: "700 12.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      cursor:
                        acceptingId === order.id || quickBlocked
                          ? 'not-allowed'
                          : 'pointer',
                    }}
                  >
                    {acceptingId === order.id ? 'Memproses...' : 'Terima'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Navigation */}
      <BottomNav role="driver" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\driver\DriverHapusAkun.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';

const DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

export default function DriverHapusAkun() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [nimConfirm, setNimConfirm] = useState('');
  const [hasActiveOrders, setHasActiveOrders] = useState(false);
  const [checkingOrders, setCheckingOrders] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const checkActiveOrders = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const active = list.some(
          (item) =>
            item.driver_id === user?.id &&
            DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
        );
        if (isMounted) {
          setHasActiveOrders(active);
        }
      } catch {
        // Server akan melakukan pengecekan ulang pada Langkah 3
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) {
          setCheckingOrders(false);
        }
      }
    };

    checkActiveOrders();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [user?.id]);

  const expectedIdentifier = user?.nim || user?.email || '';
  const isConfirmed =
    expectedIdentifier !== '' &&
    nimConfirm.trim().toLowerCase() === expectedIdentifier.trim().toLowerCase();

  const canDelete = !checkingOrders && !hasActiveOrders && isConfirmed;

  const handleDeleteAccount = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    if (hasActiveOrders) {
      setError('Selesaikan pesanan aktif terlebih dahulu sebelum menghapus akun.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isConfirmed) {
      setError('Konfirmasi NIM tidak sesuai dengan akunmu.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await api.delete('/auth/me', { data: { nim: nimConfirm.trim() } });
      logout();
      navigate('/login', { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menghapus akun.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleDeleteAccount}
      noValidate
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to="/driver/profil"
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '17px',
            letterSpacing: '-.01em',
          }}
        >
          Hapus akun
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '10px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
        }}
      >
        <Card
          variant="danger"
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '8px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
            <svg
              width="20"
              height="20"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="#B91C1C"
              strokeWidth="2.2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path d="M12 9v4M12 17h.01" />
              <path d="M10.3 3.8L1.8 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h17a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.8a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0z" />
            </svg>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
              Tindakan ini permanen
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ fontSize: '12.5px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
            Menghapus akun driver akan menghapus profil, data kendaraan, dan
            seluruh riwayat pengantaran AnJem maupun JasTip secara permanen dan
            tidak dapat dipulihkan.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {hasActiveOrders && (
          <Card
            style={{
              background: '#FEF6E7',
              boxShadow: '0 0 0 1.5px #F59E0B',
              color: '#6B4308',
              fontSize: '12.5px',
              fontWeight: 600,
            }}
          >
            Kamu masih memiliki pesanan yang sedang diantar. Selesaikan pesanan
            aktif terlebih dahulu sebelum menghapus akun.
          </Card>
        )}

        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '10px',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Konfirmasi penghapusan</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
            Ketik{' '}
            <strong style={{ color: '#0F172A' }}>{expectedIdentifier}</strong>{' '}
            di bawah ini untuk melanjutkan.
          </div>

          <Input
            variant="bordered"
            label={user?.nim ? 'Konfirmasi NIM' : 'Konfirmasi Email'}
            placeholder={expectedIdentifier}
            value={nimConfirm}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setNimConfirm(e.target.value);
              if (error) setError('');
            }}
          />
        </Card>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Bawah */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '12px 20px 20px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '10px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant="danger"
          disabled={!canDelete || submitting}
        >
          {submitting ? 'Menghapus akun...' : 'Hapus akun permanen'}
        </Button>

        <Button to="/driver/profil" variant="outline">
          Batal
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';

const HISTORY_STATUSES = ['completed', 'cancelled'];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  return (Number(value) || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatTanggal(isoString) {
  if (!isoString) return 'Hari ini';
  const date = new Date(isoString);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'Hari ini';
  return date.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

function readCache(key) {
  try {
    const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || '[]');
    return Array.isArray(cached) ? cached : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function saveCache(key, data) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Cache riwayat tidak dapat disimpan:', error);
  }
}

export default function DriverHistory() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  const cacheKey = `anjemdips:history:driver:${userId || 'guest'}`;

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState(() => readCache(cacheKey));
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetchHistory = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!userId) {
      setOrders([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    // Tampilkan cache terlebih dahulu.
    const cached = readCache(cacheKey);
    setOrders(cached);
    setLoading(false);

    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];

      const driverHistory = list.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.driver_id === userId &&
          HISTORY_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
      );

      // Respons kosong tidak boleh menghapus cache yang sudah ada.
      if (driverHistory.length > 0) {
        setOrders(driverHistory);
        saveCache(cacheKey, driverHistory);
      } else if (cached.length > 0) {
        setOrders(cached);
      } else {
        setOrders([]);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Gagal mengambil riwayat dari server:', error);
      setOrders(cached);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [userId, cacheKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchHistory();
  }, [fetchHistory]);

  const completedOrders = orders.filter(
    (item) => item.status === 'completed'
  );

  const totalEarnings = completedOrders.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.ongkir) || 0),
    0
  );

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter((item) => {
    if (filter === 'completed') return item.status === 'completed';
    if (filter === 'cancelled') return item.status === 'cancelled';
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          History
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Riwayat pengantaran dan total pendapatan.
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            background: '#E8EBEF',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            padding: '3px',
            gap: '3px',
            marginTop: '14px',
          }}
        >
          {[
            { key: 'all', label: 'Semua' },
            { key: 'completed', label: 'Selesai' },
            { key: 'cancelled', label: 'Dibatalkan' },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const active = filter === tab.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFilter(tab.key)}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  height: '36px',
                  borderRadius: '9px',
                  border: 0,
                  background: active ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                  color: active ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                  font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  boxShadow: active ? '0 1px 3px rgba(15,23,42,.12)' : 'none',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '14px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        <Card
          variant="soft"
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            alignItems: 'center',
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <div style={{ color: '#0B5F59', fontSize: '11.5px', fontWeight: 600 }}>
              Total pendapatan bersih
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '20px',
                color: '#0B4F4A',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(totalEarnings)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <Chip variant="teal">{completedOrders.length} selesai</Chip>
        </Card>

        {loading ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat riwayat pengantaran...
          </Card>
        ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              textAlign: 'center',
              padding: '24px 16px',
              color: '#5B6678',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0F172A' }}>
              Belum ada riwayat
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              Pengantaran yang sudah selesai atau dibatalkan akan tampil di
              sini.
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          filteredOrders.map((order) => {
            const isJastip = order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
            const isCompleted = order.status === 'completed';

            return (
              <Card
                key={order.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '10px',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    gap: '8px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
                      {isJastip ? 'JasTip' : 'AnJem'} Â·{' '}
                      {order.customer?.nama || 'Mahasiswa'}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11.5px' }}>
                      {formatTanggal(order.updated_at || order.created_at)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <Chip variant={isCompleted ? 'success' : 'danger'}>
                    {isCompleted ? 'Selesai' : 'Dibatalkan'}
                  </Chip>
                </div>

                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    gap: '6px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '8px',
                        height: '8px',
                        borderRadius: '99px',
                        background: '#0F766E',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
                      {order.titik_jemput}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '8px',
                        height: '8px',
                        borderRadius: '2px',
                        background: '#F59E0B',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
                      {order.titik_antar}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />

                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                    Upah diterima
                  </span>
                  <span
                    style={{
                      fontWeight: 800,
                      fontSize: '14px',
                      color: isCompleted ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                    }}
                  >
                    Rp {formatRibuan(order.ongkir)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      <BottomNav role="driver" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\driver\DriverNavigasi.jsx
