import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';

const DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatWaUrl(phone, text) {
  if (!phone) return null;
  let cleaned = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleaned.startsWith('0')) {
    cleaned = `62${cleaned.slice(1)}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${cleaned}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

export default function DriverNavigasi() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const syncOrder = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const target = order?.id
          ? list.find((item) => item.id === order.id)
          : list.find(
              (item) =>
                item.driver_id === user?.id &&
                DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
            );

        if (isMounted && target) {
          setOrder(target);
        }
      } catch {
        // Pertahankan state terakhir
      }
    };

    syncOrder();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [order?.id, user?.id]);

  const isOnWay = ['on_progress', 'on_the_way', 'in_progress'].includes(
    order?.status
  );
  const isCompleted = order?.status === 'completed';
  const isJastip = order?.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';

  const stageTitle = isCompleted
    ? 'Perjalanan selesai'
    : isOnWay
    ? 'Menuju lokasi antar'
    : isJastip
    ? 'Menuju lokasi pembelian'
    : 'Menuju titik jemput';

  const targetAddress = isOnWay
    ? order?.titik_antar || 'Titik tujuan'
    : order?.titik_jemput || 'Titik jemput';

  const activeSteps = isCompleted ? 4 : isOnWay ? 3 : 2;
  const customerName = order?.customer?.nama || 'Mahasiswa';
  const customerInitial = (customerName.trim()[0] || 'M').toUpperCase();
  const customerWa = formatWaUrl(
    order?.customer?.no_wa,
    `Halo kak ${customerName}, saya sudah di perjalanan.`
  );

  const hargaBarang = Number(order?.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;
  const ongkir = Number(order?.ongkir) || 0;
  const totalAmount = isJastip ? hargaBarang + ongkir : ongkir;

  const handleAdvanceStage = async () => {
    if (!order?.id) return;
    setError('');
    setUpdating(true);
    try {
      const nextStatus = isOnWay ? 'completed' : 'on_progress';
      const res = await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/status`, {
        status: nextStatus,
      });
      if (nextStatus === 'completed') {
        navigate('/driver/history');
      } else {
        setOrder(res.data?.data || { ...order, status: 'on_progress' });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal memperbarui status perjalanan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setUpdating(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#E9EEF0',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Peta Rute */}
      <svg
        width="390"
        height="600"
        viewBox="0 0 390 600"
        fill="none"
        aria-hidden="true"
        style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0 }}
      >
        <path
          d="M-10 460 C90 420 150 330 230 350 S350 410 400 290"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="16"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path
          d="M130 -10 C160 110 110 220 170 350 S230 520 210 610"
          stroke="#fff"
          strokeWidth="10"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <path d="M-10 170 L400 130" stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="9" />
        <rect x="310" y="370" width="70" height="70" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <rect x="18" y="220" width="80" height="90" rx="10" fill="#DDE7E4" />
        <path
          d="M120 120 C130 200 160 280 190 345"
          stroke="#0F766E"
          strokeWidth="6"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <circle
          cx="190"
          cy="345"
          r="9"
          fill="#fff"
          stroke="#F59E0B"
          strokeWidth="3"
        />
        <circle cx="190" cy="345" r="3.5" fill="#F59E0B" />
        <g transform="translate(120 120)">
          <circle r="20" fill="#0F766E" fillOpacity=".18" />
          <circle r="14" fill="#0F766E" stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="3" />
          <circle r="4" fill="#fff" />
        </g>
      </svg>

      {/* Tombol Kembali */}
      <Link
        to="/driver/pesanan"
        aria-label="Kembali"
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          left: '16px',
          top: '16px',
          width: '44px',
          height: '44px',
          borderRadius: '14px',
          background: '#fff',
          boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(15,23,42,.12)',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          color: '#0F172A',
        }}
      >
        <svg
          width="20"
          height="20"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
        </svg>
      </Link>

      {/* Pil GPS */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          right: '16px',
          top: '20px',
          background: '#fff',
          borderRadius: '99px',
          padding: '7px 12px',
          boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(15,23,42,.12)',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          fontWeight: 700,
          fontSize: '12px',
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            width: '8px',
            height: '8px',
            borderRadius: '99px',
            background: '#16A34A',
          }}
        />
        Navigasi aktif
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Sheet */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          bottom: 0,
          background: '#fff',
          borderRadius: '28px 28px 0 0',
          padding: '12px 20px 22px',
          boxShadow: '0 -8px 30px rgba(15,23,42,.1)',
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: '40px',
            height: '4px',
            borderRadius: '4px',
            background: '#D5DAE1',
            margin: '0 auto 14px',
          }}
        />

        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            alignItems: 'flex-end',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ minWidth: 0, flex: 1 }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              {stageTitle}
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '18px',
                letterSpacing: '-.02em',
                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                overflow: 'hidden',
                textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
              }}
            >
              {targetAddress}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'right', flex: 'none', marginLeft: '10px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>Estimasi</div>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
              {isOnWay ? 'Â± 6 mnt' : 'Â± 4 mnt'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Progress Bar 4 Tahap */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', marginTop: '14px' }}>
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((step) => (
            <div
              key={step}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                height: '4px',
                borderRadius: '4px',
                background: step <= activeSteps ? '#0F766E' : '#E2E6EB',
              }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Kartu Pemesan */}
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: '16px',
            display: 'flex',
            gap: '12px',
            alignItems: 'center',
            background: '#F5F6F8',
            borderRadius: '16px',
            padding: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              width: '46px',
              height: '46px',
              borderRadius: '99px',
              background: '#0F766E',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontWeight: 800,
              fontSize: '16px',
              flex: 'none',
            }}
          >
            {customerInitial}
          </div>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
              {customerName}
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              {isJastip
                ? order?.nama_barang || 'JasTip'
                : `AnJem Â· ${order?.jumlah_penumpang || 1} penumpang`}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Total tagihan
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
              Rp {formatRibuan(totalAmount)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              marginTop: '10px',
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
              textAlign: 'center',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tombol Aksi */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', marginTop: '14px' }}>
          {customerWa && (
            <a
              href={customerWa}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
              style={{
                height: '46px',
                borderRadius: '14px',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                fontWeight: 700,
                fontSize: '13px',
                textDecoration: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
                padding: '0 16px',
                background: '#fff',
                color: '#0F172A',
                boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
              }}
            >
              WhatsApp
            </a>
          )}

          {order && !isCompleted && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={updating}
              onClick={handleAdvanceStage}
              style={{
                height: '46px',
                borderRadius: '14px',
                border: 0,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                font: "700 13px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                flex: 1,
                background: isOnWay ? '#16A34A' : '#0F766E',
                color: '#fff',
                cursor: updating ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              {updating
                ? 'Memproses...'
                : isOnWay
                ? 'Selesaikan pesanan'
                : 'Mulai antar'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import { DRIVER_NEGO_INCREMENTS } from '../../mocks/driverMocks';

function formatRibuan(digitsOrNum) {
  const clean = String(digitsOrNum || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (!clean) return '';
  return Number(clean).toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export default function DriverNego() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { id } = useParams();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const baseOffer = Number(order?.ongkir) || 15000;

  const [offerDigits, setOfferDigits] = useState(String(baseOffer + 3000));
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [sentSuccess, setSentSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Jika halaman di-refresh (state hilang), ambil ulang order dari server
  useEffect(() => {
    if (order?.id || !id) return;
    let isMounted = true;

    const loadOrder = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const found = list.find((item) => String(item.id) === String(id));
        if (isMounted && found) {
          setOrder(found);
          setOfferDigits(String((Number(found.ongkir) || 15000) + 3000));
        }
      } catch {
        // Abaikan gangguan jaringan sementara
      }
    };

    loadOrder();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [id, order?.id]);

  const numericOffer = Number(offerDigits) || 0;
  const backUrl = order?.id ? `/driver/request/${order.id}` : '/driver';

  const handleAddIncrement = (inc) => {
    const next = (Number(offerDigits) || baseOffer) + inc;
    setOfferDigits(String(next));
    if (error) setError('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    if (!numericOffer || numericOffer < 2000) {
      setError('Minimal penawaran harga adalah Rp 2.000.');
      return;
    }

    if (!order?.id) {
      setError('Data pesanan tidak ditemukan. Kembali ke daftar request.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await api.post(`/orders/${order.id}/offers`, {
        offered_price: numericOffer,
        note: note.trim(),
      });
      setSentSuccess(true);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal mengirim tawaran harga.'
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      noValidate
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <Link
          to={backUrl}
          state={order ? { order } : undefined}
          aria-label="Kembali"
          style={{
            width: '44px',
            height: '44px',
            marginLeft: '-12px',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#0F172A',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="20"
            height="20"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
          </svg>
        </Link>
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 800,
            fontSize: '17px',
            letterSpacing: '-.01em',
          }}
        >
          Tawar harga
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '8px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Ringkasan Request */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '8px',
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
              {order?.customer?.nama || 'Pemesan'}
            </span>
            <span style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              Tawaran awal: <strong>Rp {formatRibuan(baseOffer)}</strong>
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: '12.5px' }}>
            {order?.titik_jemput || 'Titik jemput'} â†’{' '}
            {order?.titik_antar || 'Titik antar'}
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Input Penawaran Driver */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <label style={{ display: 'block' }}>
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#475569',
                marginBottom: '6px',
              }}
            >
              Harga yang kamu ajukan
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                height: '52px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#F5F6F8',
                padding: '0 14px',
              }}
            >
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#5B6678' }}>Rp</span>
              <input
                aria-label="Penawaran harga driver"
                inputMode="numeric"
                value={formatRibuan(offerDigits)}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setOfferDigits(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''));
                  if (error) setError('');
                }}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  border: 0,
                  outline: 0,
                  background: 'transparent',
                  font: "800 20px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  color: '#0F766E',
                  minWidth: 0,
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </label>

          {/* Preset Kenaikan Cepat */}
          <div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#5B6678',
                marginBottom: '6px',
              }}
            >
              Tambah cepat dari tawaran pemesan
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
              {DRIVER_NEGO_INCREMENTS.map((inc) => (
                <button
                  key={inc}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleAddIncrement(inc)}
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    height: '40px',
                    borderRadius: '11px',
                    border: 0,
                    background: '#fff',
                    boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                    font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                    color: '#0F172A',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}
                >
                  + Rp {formatRibuan(inc)}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <Input
            label="Alasan / catatan untuk pemesan (opsional)"
            placeholder="Misal: Jarak agak jauh atau hujan"
            value={note}
            onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Card>

        {/* Info Nego */}
        <Card
          variant="soft"
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            gap: '10px',
            alignItems: 'flex-start',
          }}
        >
          <svg
            width="18"
            height="18"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="#0F766E"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            aria-hidden="true"
            style={{ flex: 'none', marginTop: '1px' }}
          >
            <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
            <path d="M12 8h.01M11 12h1v5h1" />
          </svg>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0B4F4A' }}>
            Pemesan akan menerima penawaranmu dan dapat langsung menyetujui
            atau mengajukan harga balik.
          </div>
        </Card>

        {sentSuccess && (
          <Card
            style={{
              background: '#DDF3E4',
              color: '#14532D',
              fontWeight: 700,
              fontSize: '12.5px',
            }}
          >
            Penawaran Rp {formatRibuan(numericOffer)} berhasil dikirim ke
            pemesan! Menunggu konfirmasi pemesan.
          </Card>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Bawah */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '12px 20px 20px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '10px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        {sentSuccess ? (
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="primary"
            onClick={() => navigate('/driver')}
          >
            Kembali ke beranda
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={submitting}>
            {submitting
              ? 'Mengirim tawaran...'
              : `Kirim tawaran Rp ${formatRibuan(numericOffer)}`}
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\driver\DriverPesanan.jsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';
import { waktuLabel } from '../../utils/format';

const DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES = [
  'accepted',
  'on_progress',
  'on_the_way',
  'in_progress',
];

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

function formatWaUrl(phone, text) {
  if (!phone) return null;
  let cleaned = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleaned.startsWith('0')) {
    cleaned = `62${cleaned.slice(1)}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${cleaned}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

export default function DriverPesanan() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [updatingId, setUpdatingId] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const fetchDriverOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/orders');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
      const activeList = list.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.driver_id === user?.id &&
          DRIVER_ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(item.status)
      );
      setOrders(activeList);
      setError('');
    } catch {
      setError('Koneksi terputus. Menampilkan data pesanan terakhir yang berhasil dimuat.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDriverOrders();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchDriverOrders, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [fetchDriverOrders]);

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (orderId, nextStatus) => {
    setError('');
    setUpdatingId(orderId);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/orders/${orderId}/status`, {
        status: nextStatus,
      });
      await fetchDriverOrders();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal memperbarui status pesanan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setUpdatingId(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          Pesanan
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Pesanan aktif yang sedang kamu tangani.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '16px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat pesanan aktif...
          </Card>
        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              alignItems: 'center',
              textAlign: 'center',
              gap: '8px',
              padding: '24px 16px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
              Belum ada pesanan aktif
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
              Ambil request masuk dari Beranda Driver untuk mulai mengantar.
            </div>
            <Link
              to="/driver"
              style={{
                marginTop: '6px',
                height: '40px',
                padding: '0 18px',
                borderRadius: '12px',
                background: '#0F766E',
                color: '#fff',
                fontWeight: 700,
                fontSize: '13px',
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                textDecoration: 'none',
              }}
            >
              Lihat request masuk
            </Link>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          orders.map((order) => {
            const isJastip = order.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';
            const isMotor = order.jenis_kendaraan === 'motor';
            const isOnWay = ['on_progress', 'on_the_way', 'in_progress'].includes(
              order.status
            );

            const customerName = order.customer?.nama || 'Mahasiswa';
            const customerWa = formatWaUrl(
              order.customer?.no_wa,
              `Halo kak ${customerName}, saya driver AnjemDips yang mengambil pesananmu.`
            );

            const hargaBarang = Number(order.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;
            const ongkir = Number(order.ongkir) || 0;
            const totalTagihan = isJastip ? hargaBarang + ongkir : ongkir;

            return (
              <Card
                key={order.id}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '12px',
                }}
              >
                {/* Header Kartu */}
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '10px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: '38px',
                      height: '38px',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      background: isJastip ? '#FDE9B8' : '#E6F4F2',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      color: isJastip ? '#6B4308' : '#0F766E',
                      flex: 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    {isJastip ? (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <path d="M5 8h14l-1 12H6z" />
                        <path d="M9 8a3 3 0 0 1 6 0" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : isMotor ? (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <circle cx="6" cy="16" r="3" />
                        <circle cx="18" cy="16" r="3" />
                        <path d="M6 16l4-7h5l3 7M9 9H7" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : (
                      <svg
                        width="20"
                        height="20"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="1.8"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <path d="M4 16v-4l2-5h12l2 5v4zM4 16v3M20 16v3" />
                      </svg>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>{customerName}</div>
                    <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                      {isJastip
                        ? `JasTip Â· ${order.nama_barang || 'Titip barang'}${
                            order.waktu_berangkat === 'jadwal'
                              ? ` Â· ${waktuLabel(order)}`
                              : ''
                          }`
                        : `AnJem Â· ${order.jumlah_penumpang || 1} penumpang${
                            order.waktu_berangkat === 'jadwal'
                              ? ` Â· ${waktuLabel(order)}`
                              : ''
                          }`}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <Chip variant="teal">
                    {isOnWay ? 'Di jalan' : 'Menjemput'}
                  </Chip>
                </div>

                {/* Rute */}
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    gap: '8px',
                    paddingLeft: '2px',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      gap: '10px',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '10px',
                        height: '10px',
                        borderRadius: '99px',
                        background: '#0F766E',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{order.titik_jemput}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      gap: '10px',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      style={{
                        width: '10px',
                        height: '10px',
                        borderRadius: '3px',
                        background: '#F59E0B',
                        flex: 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{order.titik_antar}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Progress Bar 4 Tahap */}
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    gap: '6px',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '4px',
                      borderRadius: '4px',
                      background: '#0F766E',
                    }}
                  />
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '4px',
                      borderRadius: '4px',
                      background: '#0F766E',
                    }}
                  />
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '4px',
                      borderRadius: '4px',
                      background: isOnWay ? '#0F766E' : '#E2E6EB',
                    }}
                  />
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '4px',
                      borderRadius: '4px',
                      background: '#E2E6EB',
                    }}
                  />
                </div>

                <div
                  style={{
                    color: '#5B6678',
                    fontSize: '11.5px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  }}
                >
                  <span>Diterima</span>
                  <span
                    style={
                      !isOnWay
                        ? { color: '#0F766E', fontWeight: 700 }
                        : undefined
                    }
                  >
                    Menjemput
                  </span>
                  <span
                    style={
                      isOnWay
                        ? { color: '#0F766E', fontWeight: 700 }
                        : undefined
                    }
                  >
                    Di jalan
                  </span>
                  <span>Selesai</span>
                </div>

                {/* Rincian Tarif */}
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    background: '#F5F6F8',
                    borderRadius: '12px',
                    padding: '10px 12px',
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                    {isJastip
                      ? `Upah Rp ${formatRibuan(ongkir)} + barang Rp ${formatRibuan(hargaBarang)}`
                      : 'Upah perjalanan'}
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0F766E' }}>
                    Rp {formatRibuan(totalTagihan)}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Tombol Tahap Perjalanan Utama */}
                {!isOnWay ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={updatingId === order.id}
                    onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(order.id, 'on_progress')}
                    style={{
                      height: '46px',
                      borderRadius: '14px',
                      border: 0,
                      background: '#0F766E',
                      color: '#fff',
                      font: "700 13.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      cursor:
                        updatingId === order.id ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                      width: '100%',
                    }}
                  >
                    {updatingId === order.id
                      ? 'Memperbarui...'
                      : isJastip
                      ? 'Barang dibeli Â· Mulai antar'
                      : 'Sudah jemput Â· Mulai antar'}
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={updatingId === order.id}
                    onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(order.id, 'completed')}
                    style={{
                      height: '46px',
                      borderRadius: '14px',
                      border: 0,
                      background: '#16A34A',
                      color: '#fff',
                      font: "700 13.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      cursor:
                        updatingId === order.id ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                      width: '100%',
                    }}
                  >
                    {updatingId === order.id
                      ? 'Menyelesaikan...'
                      : 'Selesaikan pesanan'}
                  </button>
                )}

                {/* Tombol Peta, WhatsApp & Batalkan */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <Link
                    to="/driver/navigasi"
                    state={{ order }}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '40px',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      background: '#fff',
                      boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #D5DAE1',
                      color: '#0F172A',
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      fontSize: '12.5px',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      textDecoration: 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    Peta
                  </Link>

                  {customerWa && (
                    <a
                      href={customerWa}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        height: '40px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        background: '#E6F4F2',
                        color: '#0B5F59',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: '12.5px',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        textDecoration: 'none',
                      }}
                    >
                      WhatsApp
                    </a>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={updatingId === order.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (
                        window.confirm(
                          'Yakin batalkan pesanan ini? Pemesan akan diberi tahu.'
                        )
                      ) {
                        handleUpdateStatus(order.id, 'cancelled');
                      }
                    }}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '40px',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      border: 0,
                      background: '#fff',
                      boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1.5px #F3C4C4',
                      color: '#B91C1C',
                      font: "700 12.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      cursor:
                        updatingId === order.id ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    }}
                  >
                    Batalkan
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Navigation */}
      <BottomNav role="driver" />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import Input from '../../components/Input';
import BottomNav from '../../components/BottomNav';
import { getDriverVehicleProfile } from '../../mocks/driverMocks';

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export default function DriverProfil() {
  const { user, logout, updateUser } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const isOnline = Boolean(user?.is_online);
  const initialVehicle = getDriverVehicleProfile(user);

  const [vehicle, setVehicle] = useState(initialVehicle);
  const [editingVehicle, setEditingVehicle] = useState(!initialVehicle.lengkap);
  const [jenisKendaraan, setJenisKendaraan] = useState(
    initialVehicle.jenis_kendaraan || 'motor'
  );
  const [platNomor, setPlatNomor] = useState(initialVehicle.plat_nomor || '');
  const [kapasitas, setKapasitas] = useState(
    String(initialVehicle.kapasitas_penumpang || 4)
  );
  const [savingVehicle, setSavingVehicle] = useState(false);
  const [vehicleError, setVehicleError] = useState('');

  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ completed: 0, earnings: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const loadStats = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const completedList = list.filter(
          (item) =>
            item.driver_id === user?.id && item.status === 'completed'
        );
        const earnings = completedList.reduce(
          (sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.ongkir) || 0),
          0
        );
        if (isMounted) {
          setStats({ completed: completedList.length, earnings });
        }
      } catch {
        // Abaikan gangguan jaringan sementara
      }
    };

    loadStats();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [user?.id]);

  const handleSaveVehicle = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setVehicleError('');
    setSavingVehicle(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.patch('/driver/vehicle', {
        jenis_kendaraan: jenisKendaraan,
        plat_nomor: platNomor,
        kapasitas_penumpang: Number(kapasitas),
      });
      const saved = res.data.user;
      updateUser(saved);
      setVehicle(getDriverVehicleProfile(saved));
      setEditingVehicle(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setVehicleError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menyimpan data kendaraan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setSavingVehicle(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate('/login', { replace: true });
  };

  const displayName = user?.nama || 'Driver';
  const initial = (displayName.trim()[0] || 'D').toUpperCase();

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        height: '844px',
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ padding: '20px 20px 0', flex: 'none' }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: '20px',
            fontWeight: 800,
            letterSpacing: '-.02em',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          Profil
        </h1>
        <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Data driver dan pengaturan kendaraan.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '16px 20px 84px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
        }}
      >
        {/* Kartu Identitas */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '14px',
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              width: '54px',
              height: '54px',
              borderRadius: '99px',
              background: '#0F766E',
              color: '#fff',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontWeight: 800,
              fontSize: '20px',
              flex: 'none',
            }}
          >
            {initial}
          </div>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '16px',
                letterSpacing: '-.01em',
              }}
            >
              {displayName}
            </div>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
              {user?.nim ? `NIM ${user.nim}` : user?.email || '-'}
            </div>
          </div>
          <Chip variant={isOnline ? 'success' : 'off'}>
            {isOnline ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}
          </Chip>
        </Card>

        {/* Statistik Driver */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 14px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Trip selesai
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '20px',
                color: '#0F766E',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              {stats.completed}
            </div>
          </Card>

          <Card style={{ flex: 1, padding: '12px 14px' }}>
            <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11px' }}>
              Total pendapatan
            </div>
            <div
              style={{
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '18px',
                color: '#0F172A',
                marginTop: '2px',
              }}
            >
              Rp {formatRibuan(stats.earnings)}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* Kartu Kendaraan Driver */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '12px',
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '14px' }}>Kendaraan</div>
            {!editingVehicle && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEditingVehicle(true)}
                style={{
                  border: 0,
                  background: 'transparent',
                  color: '#0F766E',
                  font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                Ubah
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {editingVehicle ? (
            <form
              onSubmit={handleSaveVehicle}
              style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px' }}
            >
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  background: '#F1F3F6',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  padding: '3px',
                  gap: '3px',
                }}
              >
                {['motor', 'mobil'].map((type) => (
                  <button
                    key={type}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setJenisKendaraan(type)}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: '36px',
                      borderRadius: '9px',
                      border: 0,
                      background:
                        jenisKendaraan === type ? '#fff' : 'transparent',
                      color:
                        jenisKendaraan === type ? '#0F766E' : '#5B6678',
                      font: "700 12px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,sans-serif",
                      textTransform: 'capitalize',
                      cursor: 'pointer',
                    }}
                  >
                    {type}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Input
                label="Plat nomor"
                placeholder="H 1234 AB"
                value={platNomor}
                onChange={(e) => setPlatNomor(e.target.value)}
              />

              {jenisKendaraan === 'mobil' && (
                <Input
                  label="Kapasitas penumpang"
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={8}
                  value={kapasitas}
                  onChange={(e) => setKapasitas(e.target.value)}
                />
              )}

              {vehicleError && (
                <p role="alert" style={{ margin: 0, color: '#B91C1C', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                  {vehicleError}
                </p>
              )}

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  height={40}
                  onClick={() => setEditingVehicle(false)}
                  style={{ flex: 1 }}
                >
                  Batal
                </Button>
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  variant="primary"
                  height={40}
                  disabled={savingVehicle}
                  style={{ flex: 1 }}
                >
                  {savingVehicle ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>Jenis kendaraan</span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'capitalize' }}>
                  {vehicle.jenis_kendaraan || 'Belum diisi'}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />

              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                }}
              >
                <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>Plat nomor</span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{vehicle.plat_nomor || 'Belum diisi'}</span>
              </div>

              {vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil' && (
                <>
                  <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      justifyContent: 'space-between',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>
                      Kapasitas penumpang

                      {vehicle.kapasitas_penumpang} orang
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

          )}
        </Card>

        {/* Kartu Data Akun */}
        <Card
          style={{
            display: 'flex',

FILE: frontend\src\pages\driver\DriverRequestDetail.jsx
==================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../../api/axios';
import Card from '../../components/Card';
import Chip from '../../components/Chip';
import Button from '../../components/Button';
import { getDriverVehicleProfile } from '../../mocks/driverMocks';

function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export default function DriverRequestDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [order, setOrder] = useState(() => location.state?.order || null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!location.state?.order);
  const [accepting, setAccepting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [customerOffer, setCustomerOffer] = useState(null);
  const [acceptingOffer, setAcceptingOffer] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const loadOrder = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/orders');
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const found = list.find((item) => String(item.id) === String(id));
        if (isMounted && found) {
          setOrder(found);
        }
      } catch {
        // Gunakan state yang sudah diteruskan jika jaringan lambat
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    loadOrder();
    const interval = setInterval(loadOrder, 3000);
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [id]);

  // Pantau tawaran balik dari pemesan
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const loadOffers = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get(`/orders/${id}/offers`);
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.data) ? res.data.data : [];
        const latest = list[0];
        if (isMounted) {
          setCustomerOffer(
            latest &&
              latest.offered_by === 'customer' &&
              latest.status === 'pending'
              ? latest
              : null
          );
        }
      } catch {
        // Abaikan gangguan jaringan sementara
      }
    };

    loadOffers();
    const interval = setInterval(loadOffers, 5000);
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [id]);

  const vehicle = getDriverVehicleProfile(user);
  const isJastip = order?.jenis_layanan === 'jastip';

  const driverCapacity =
    vehicle.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil'
      ? Number(vehicle.kapasitas_penumpang) || 4
      : 1;
  const requestedPassengers = Number(order?.jumlah_penumpang) || 1;
  const isOverCapacity = !isJastip && requestedPassengers > driverCapacity;
  const excessPassengers = Math.max(0, requestedPassengers - driverCapacity);

  const hargaBarang = Number(order?.perkiraan_harga_barang) || 0;
  const ongkir = Number(order?.ongkir) || 0;
  const perkiraanTotalJastip = hargaBarang + ongkir;

  const customerName = order?.customer?.nama || 'Mahasiswa';
  const customerInitial = (customerName.trim()[0] || 'M').toUpperCase();

  const handleAcceptOrder = async () => {
    if (!order?.id) return;
    setError('');
    setAccepting(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.patch(`/orders/${order.id}/accept`);
      navigate('/driver/pesanan', {
        state: { order: res.data?.data || order },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
          'Pesanan ini sudah diambil driver lain atau tidak tersedia.'
      );
    } finally {
      setAccepting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAcceptCustomerOffer = async () => {
    if (!order?.id || !customerOffer) return;
    setError('');
    setAcceptingOffer(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.patch(
        `/orders/${order.id}/offers/${customerOffer.id}/accept`
      );
      navigate('/driver/pesanan', {
        state: { order: res.data?.data || order },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menyetujui tawaran pemesan.'
      );
    } finally {
      setAcceptingOffer(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="pwa-shell"
      style={{
        minHeight: '844px',
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        background: '#F5F6F8',
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        fontSize: '13px',
        lineHeight: 1.4,
        color: '#0F172A',
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          padding: '14px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          height: '52px',
          flex: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
          <Link
            to="/driver"
            aria-label="Kembali"
            style={{
              width: '44px',
              height: '44px',
              marginLeft: '-12px',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              color: '#0F172A',
            }}
          >
            <svg
              width="20"
              height="20"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path d="M15 5l-7 7 7 7" />
            </svg>
          </Link>
          <div
            style={{
              fontWeight: 800,
              fontSize: '17px',
              letterSpacing: '-.01em',
            }}
          >
            Detail request
          </div>
        </div>

        {order && (
          <Chip variant={isJastip ? 'warning' : 'teal'}>
            {isJastip
              ? 'JasTip'
              : `AnJem Â· ${order.jenis_kendaraan === 'mobil' ? 'Mobil' : 'Motor'}`}
          </Chip>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Konten Utama */}
      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          padding: '8px 20px 0',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: '12px',
        }}
      >
        {loading && !order ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Memuat detail request...
          </Card>
        ) : !order ? (
          <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#5B6678' }}>
            Request tidak ditemukan atau sudah diambil.
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <>
            {/* Kartu Profil Pemesan */}
            <Card style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '46px',
                  height: '46px',
                  borderRadius: '99px',
                  background: '#0F766E',
                  color: '#fff',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  fontSize: '16px',
                  flex: 'none',
                }}
              >
                {customerInitial}
              </div>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '15px' }}>
                  {customerName}
                </div>
                <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                  {order.customer?.nim
                    ? `NIM ${order.customer.nim}`
                    : 'Pemesan kampus'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <Chip variant="neutral">
                {order.waktu_berangkat === 'jadwal' ? 'Jadwal' : 'Sekarang'}
              </Chip>
            </Card>

            {/* Kartu Rute */}
            <Card
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '10px',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>Rute pengantaran</div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <span
                  style={{
                    width: '10px',
                    height: '10px',
                    borderRadius: '99px',
                    background: '#0F766E',
                    flex: 'none',
                  }}
                />
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#5B6678' }}>
                    {isJastip ? 'Beli / ambil di' : 'Titik jemput'}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{order.titik_jemput}</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <span
                  style={{
                    width: '10px',
                    height: '10px',
                    borderRadius: '3px',
                    background: '#F59E0B',
                    flex: 'none',
                  }}
                />
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#5B6678' }}>
                    {isJastip ? 'Antar ke' : 'Tujuan'}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{order.titik_antar}</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {order.catatan && (
                <>
                  <div style={{ height: '1px', background: '#EEF0F3' }} />
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#475569' }}>
                    <strong>Catatan:</strong> {order.catatan}
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </Card>

            {/* Kartu Detail Khusus AnJem (Kapasitas) vs JasTip (Barang) */}
            {isJastip ? (
              <Card
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '10px',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>Detail titipan barang</div>
                <div
                  style={{
                    background: '#F5F6F8',
                    borderRadius: '12px',
                    padding: '10px 12px',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                  }}
                >
                  {order.nama_barang || 'Titipan barang mahasiswa'}
                </div>

                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: '#5B6678' }}>
                    Perkiraan harga barang
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
                    Rp {formatRibuan(hargaBarang)}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    background: '#FEF6E7',
                    borderRadius: '12px',
                    padding: '10px 12px',
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: '#6B4308', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                    Perkiraan total (barang + upah)
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#4A2C03' }}>
                    Rp {formatRibuan(perkiraanTotalJastip)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </Card>
            ) : (
              <Card
                variant={isOverCapacity ? 'danger' : 'default'}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '10px',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      fontWeight: 800,
                      color: isOverCapacity ? '#7F1D1D' : '#0F172A',
                    }}
                  >
                    Cek kapasitas kendaraan
                  </div>
                  <Chip variant={isOverCapacity ? 'danger' : 'success'}>
                    {isOverCapacity
                      ? `Kelebihan ${excessPassengers} orang`
                      : 'Muat'}
                  </Chip>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      background: isOverCapacity ? '#fff' : '#F5F6F8',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      padding: '10px 12px',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#5B6678' }}>
                      Kapasitasmu
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '18px' }}>
                      {driverCapacity} org
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      flex: 1,
                      background: isOverCapacity ? '#FDECEC' : '#DDF3E4',
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      padding: '10px 12px',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        color: isOverCapacity ? '#991B1B' : '#14532D',
                      }}
                    >
                      Permintaan
                    </div>
                    <div
                      style={{
                        fontWeight: 800,
                        fontSize: '18px',
                        color: isOverCapacity ? '#991B1B' : '#14532D',
                      }}
                    >
                      {requestedPassengers} org
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {isOverCapacity && (
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#7F1D1D' }}>
                    Jumlah penumpang melebihi kapasitas kendaraanmu. Kamu bisa
                    menawar harga ekstra lewat tombol â€œTawar hargaâ€.
                  </div>
                )}
              </Card>
            )}

            {/* Kartu Tawaran Harga Pemesan */}
            <Card
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
              }}
            >
              <div>
                <div style={{ color: '#5B6678', fontSize: '11.5px' }}>
                  {isJastip
                    ? 'Upah driver ditawarkan'
                    : 'Tarif yang ditawarkan pemesan'}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '11.5px', color: '#5B6678' }}>
                  Setujui langsung atau ajukan harga baru
                </div>
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  fontSize: '20px',
                  color: '#0F766E',
                  letterSpacing: '-.02em',
                }}
              >
                Rp {formatRibuan(ongkir)}
              </div>
            </Card>
          </>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            style={{
              borderRadius: '12px',
              background: '#FEF2F2',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: '#B91C1C',
            }}
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tombol Aksi Bawah */}
      {order && (
        <div
          style={{
            padding: '12px 20px 20px',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: '10px',
            flex: 'none',
          }}
        >
          {order.status !== 'pending' || order.driver_id ? (
            <Card variant="soft">
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0B4F4A' }}>
                {order.driver_id === user?.id
                  ? 'Harga sudah disepakati'
                  : 'Pesanan ini sudah tidak tersedia'}
              </div>
              {order.driver_id === user?.id ? (
                <>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0B5F59', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                    Harga final: Rp {formatRibuan(order.ongkir)}
                  </div>
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant="primary"
                    height={44}
                    onClick={() => navigate('/driver/pesanan', { state: { order } })}
                  >
                    Lihat pesanan aktif
                  </Button>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#5B6678', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                  Muat ulang daftar request untuk melihat pesanan lain.
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <>
          {customerOffer && (
            <Card
              variant="soft"
              style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}
            >
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0B4F4A' }}>
                Pemesan menawar balik Rp{' '}
                {formatRibuan(customerOffer.offered_price)}
              </div>
              {customerOffer.adjusted_passengers ? (
                <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0B5F59' }}>
                  Penumpang disesuaikan menjadi{' '}
                  {customerOffer.adjusted_passengers} orang.
                </div>
              ) : null}
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="primary"
                height={44}
                disabled={acceptingOffer}
                onClick={handleAcceptCustomerOffer}
              >
                {acceptingOffer
                  ? 'Menyetujui...'
                  : `Setuju Rp ${formatRibuan(customerOffer.offered_price)}`}
              </Button>
            </Card>
          )}

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="primary"
            disabled={accepting || isOverCapacity || !user?.is_online}
            onClick={handleAcceptOrder}
          >
            {accepting
              ? 'Menerima pesanan...'
              : isOverCapacity
              ? 'Melebihi kapasitas, silakan tawar'
              : !user?.is_online
              ? 'Aktifkan status ON dulu'
              : `Terima Rp ${formatRibuan(ongkir)}`}
          </Button>

          <Button
            to={`/driver/nego/${order.id}`}
            state={{ order }}
            variant="outline"
          >
            Tawar harga
          </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\utils\format.js

// Helper format bersama untuk halaman pemesan & driver.

export const MAX_PENUMPANG_MOBIL = 8;

export function formatRibuan(value) {
  const num = Number(value) || 0;
  return num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
}

export function formatWaUrl(phone, text) {
  if (!phone) return null;
  let cleaned = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleaned.startsWith('0')) {
    cleaned = `62${cleaned.slice(1)}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${cleaned}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

// Contoh hasil: "Sen, 24 Nov Â· 19:30"
export function formatJadwal(isoString) {
  if (!isoString) return '';
  const date = new Date(isoString);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';

  const tanggal = date.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    weekday: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  });
  const jam = date
    .toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      hour12: false,
    })
    .replace('.', ':');

  return `${tanggal} Â· ${jam}`;
}

// "Sekarang" atau "Jadwal Sen, 24 Nov Â· 19:30"
export function waktuLabel(order) {
  if (order?.waktu_berangkat === 'jadwal') {
    const jadwal = formatJadwal(order.jadwal_at);
    return jadwal ? `Jadwal ${jadwal}` : 'Jadwal';
  }
  return 'Sekarang';
}
